① The photograph evokes a summer that three of them remember differently.
= 那张照片唤起一个夏天，他们三个记得的却各不相同。
evoke a memory = 唤起记忆
evoke a response = 引起反应
② The proposal evoked more sympathy than support.
= 那个提议引来的同情多过支持。
evoke sympathy = 引起同情

evolution
核心：三支。一是（生物）进化：物种世代变化的过程；
二是（渐进的）发展、演变；三是（正式）形势的演进。不可数。
① Evolution has no plan and the eye gets used as proof of one.
= 进化没有计划，可人们偏拿眼睛当计划的证据。
evolution = 进化
the theory of evolution = 进化论
② The evolution of that shop from a stall took eleven years.
= 那家店从一个摊位演变过来，用了十一年。
the evolution of something = 某物的演变

evolutionary
核心：两支。一是进化的：跟生物进化有关；
二是渐进的：说变化一步步来，跟 revolutionary 突变式的相对。
① Evolutionary pressure explains the beak and not the song.
= 进化压力解释得了鸟喙，解释不了鸣声。
evolutionary pressure = 进化压力
② The change was evolutionary and nobody noticed until it was finished.
= 那种变化是渐进的，等它完成了才有人察觉。
an evolutionary change = 渐进式的变化
副词 evolutionarily：从进化的角度看：修饰整句或形容词，多见于生物学写作。
③ Evolutionarily, a bright colour is a bargain and a risk at once.
= 从进化上说，鲜艳的颜色既是划算的买卖，也是风险。
evolutionarily = 从进化上说
④ The behaviour is evolutionarily old and culturally recent.
= 那种行为在进化上很古老，在文化上却很晚近。
be evolutionarily old = 在进化上很古老
构词：evolutionary 加 -ly

evolutionism
核心：进化论主张：认为生物或社会文化循进化规律发展的一整套观点；
社会科学里用它多带批评意味。不可数。
① Nineteenth-century evolutionism ranked societies and called it science.
= 十九世纪那套进化论主张给各社会排座次，还管这叫科学。
evolutionism = 进化论主张
② Evolutionism in anthropology was abandoned and keeps coming back.
= 人类学里的进化论主张早被弃用，却总是卷土重来。
social evolutionism = 社会进化论

evolutionist
核心：进化论者：主张用进化来解释生物或社会变化的人；也作形容词。可数。
① The evolutionists won the argument and lost two school boards.
= 进化论者赢了辩论，输掉了两个学区董事会。
an evolutionist = 进化论者
② An evolutionist account of the ritual explains everything except the words.
= 用进化论解释那套仪式，除了那些词句什么都解释得通。
an evolutionist account = 进化论式的解释
构词：evolution 加 -ist

evolve
核心：三支。一是（生物）进化：物种世代演变；二是（逐渐）演变成；
三是（正式）逐步形成（规矩、体系）。
① Birds evolved from dinosaurs and kept the feathers.
= 鸟从恐龙演化而来，把羽毛留了下来。
evolve from something = 由某物进化而来
② The market stall evolved into a shop and then into three.
= 那个市场摊位先变成一家店，后来成了三家。
evolve into something = 演变成某物
③ The rules evolved over twenty years and nobody wrote them down.
= 那些规矩是二十年里慢慢形成的，谁也没写下来。
evolve = 逐步形成

ewe
核心：母羊：成年的雌绵羊，公的叫 ram。可数。
读音跟 you 一样，也跟紫杉 yew 同音。
① The ewe had twins and refused the second one for a day.
= 那只母羊生了双胞胎，头一天不肯认第二只。
a ewe = 母羊
② A ewe knows her own lamb by voice in a field of two hundred.
= 两百只羊的场子里，母羊靠声音就认得出自己那只羔。
ewes = 母羊（复数）

ewer
核心：（旧式）盥洗壶、大口水壶：从前配脸盆用的宽口壶。可数。
① The ewer and the basin sit on the washstand and hold dust.
= 那把水壶和脸盆摆在盥洗架上，装的是灰。
a ewer = 盥洗壶
② A ewer without its basin is worth about a third as much.
= 一把没了脸盆的水壶，价钱只剩三分之一左右。
an earthenware ewer = 陶制盥洗壶

eww
核心：（口语）呃、真恶心：表示嫌恶的感叹，多见于短信与对话；
也拼 ew、ewww，拉长的写法表示更嫌弃。
① Eww — there is something at the bottom of that mug.
= 呃，那只杯子底下有东西。
eww = 呃（表示嫌恶）
② She said eww and finished the sandwich anyway.
= 她说了声「好恶心」，那份三明治照样吃完了。
eww = 好恶心

ex
核心：三支。一是（口语）前任：前夫、前妻或前男女友，可数，复数 exes；
二是写成 ex- 作前缀，表示「原来的」：her ex-husband、ex-service、ex-directory；
三是照搬拉丁语的一组书面说法：ex officio、ex gratia、ex cathedra、ex libris。
① Her ex still comes to the family Christmas and carves the meat.
= 她的前任还来过家里的圣诞节，负责切肉。
an ex = 前任
② The two exes share a dog and a calendar.
= 那两位前任共养一条狗，共用一份日程表。
exes = 前任们
③ The dean sits on the panel ex officio and votes only to break a tie.
= 教务长以职务身份列席评审组，只在票数相同时投票。
ex officio = 因职位而当然（列席、任职）
an ex gratia payment = 通融性支付（不代表认责）
a ruling ex cathedra = 以权威身份作出的裁断
④ An ex libris label on the flyleaf gives a name that the catalogue does not.
= 扉页上的藏书票写着一个名字，目录里可查不到。
ex libris = 藏书票上的「某某藏书」
⑤ The number has been ex-directory since a reporter found it in 1998.
= 那个号码自 1998 年被记者翻到之后，就不再列进电话簿。
be ex-directory = 号码不列入电话簿
⑥ The club is for ex-service families and the bar opens at six.
= 那家俱乐部只对退役军人家庭开放，酒吧六点开门。
ex-service = 退役的、退伍军人的
an ex-serviceman = 退伍男军人
an ex-servicewoman = 退伍女军人

exacerbate
核心：（正式）使恶化、加剧：让本来就不好的情况更糟。名词 exacerbation。
注意别混：exacerbate 是让坏事更坏，exasperate 是把人惹恼，拼写只差一点。
① The apology exacerbated the row and added two more people to it.
= 那次道歉把争吵闹得更凶，还多卷进两个人。
exacerbate something = 使某事恶化
② Cold air exacerbates the cough and the tablets do nothing for it.
= 冷空气加重那种咳嗽，药片一点用没有。
exacerbate a problem = 使问题加剧

exacerbation
核心：两支。一是（正式）加剧、恶化；二是（医学）病情急性加重。可数不可数都行。
① The exacerbation was gradual and the collapse was not.
= 恶化是渐进的，垮下来却不是。
the exacerbation of something = 某事的加剧
② Two exacerbations in one winter changed the whole treatment plan.
= 一个冬天里两次急性加重，整个治疗方案就改了。
an exacerbation = 一次急性加重

exact
核心：形容词与动词。形容词是确切的、精确的；
动词是（正式）索取、强求：exact a price。副词 exactly，名词 exactness 与 exactitude。
① Nobody knows the exact number and three people quote it.
= 确切数字谁也不知道，倒有三个人引用它。
the exact number = 确切的数字
the exact opposite = 恰恰相反的东西
② The delay exacted a price that the contract did not mention.
= 那次延误索去的代价，合同里并没写。
exact a price = 索取代价
副词 exactly：三支。一是确切地、正好；二是（回应）正是、没错； 三是（否定里）不完全：not exactly。
③ The train leaves at exactly seven and the clock on the platform is slow.
= 火车七点整开，站台上那个钟走得慢。
exactly = 正好、确切地
exactly the same = 一模一样
④ 'So nobody checked?' 'Exactly.'
= 「这么说没人核对过？」「正是。」
exactly = 正是、没错
⑤ It is not exactly a problem and it is not exactly fine either.
= 这算不上问题，也说不上没事。
not exactly = 不完全是

exacting
核心：要求严格的、费神费力的：说人标准高，也说工作耗神。
① An exacting editor saves a writer from four bad sentences a page.
= 一位要求严格的编辑，能让作者每页少四句烂句子。
an exacting editor = 要求严格的编辑
② The work is exacting and pays less than the work next to it.
= 那份活儿费神，工钱比旁边那份还少。
exacting standards = 严苛的标准

exaction
核心：（正式）强征、硬要来的款项或代价。可数不可数都行。
① The exactions of that century are recorded in the parish accounts.
= 那个世纪的种种苛捐，都记在教区的账上。
an exaction = 强征的款项
② Exaction by threat is a crime and by contract is a fee.
= 靠威胁索取是犯罪，写进合同就成了收费。
exaction = 强征、索取

exactitude
核心：（正式）精确、严谨：说做事与用词分毫不差。不可数。
① The exactitude of the drawings is why the building fits together.
= 那些图纸分毫不差，楼才拼得起来。
exactitude = 精确、严谨
② Exactitude about dates matters more than eloquence in that job.
= 干那一行，日期上的精确比口才要紧。
with exactitude = 精确地

exactness
核心：准确、精确：跟 exactitude 意思相近，用起来稍口语些。不可数。
① The exactness of the measurement matters and the units matter more.
= 测得准要紧，单位对不对更要紧。
exactness = 准确
② She reported the words with an exactness nobody had asked for.
= 她把那些话一字不差地转述出来，可没人要求她这样。
the exactness of something = 某物的精确
构词：exact 加 -ness

exaggerate
核心：两支。一是夸大、言过其实：把事情说得比实际厉害；
二是（外观上）使显得过分。名词 exaggeration。
① He exaggerates the distance to the station and the walk is long enough as it is.
= 他把到车站那段路说得更远，其实那段路本来就够远的了。
exaggerate something = 夸大某事
② Nobody exaggerates the length of a queue they have not stood in themselves.
= 没有人会把自己没排过的队伍说得更长。
exaggerate = 言过其实

exaggerated
核心：夸大的、言过其实的：说说法、担心或动作过了头。副词 exaggeratedly。
① The reports were exaggerated and the fire was real.
= 那些报道夸大了，火倒是真的。
be exaggerated = 被夸大
an exaggerated claim = 夸大的说法
② He made an exaggerated bow and knocked over the water jug.
= 他夸张地鞠了一躬，把水壶碰倒了。
an exaggerated gesture = 夸张的动作
副词 exaggeratedly：夸张地、过分地：修饰动作、语气与表情。
③ She spoke exaggeratedly slowly and everybody understood the first time.
= 她说得夸张地慢，人人头一遍就听懂了。
exaggeratedly slowly = 夸张地慢
④ The sign is exaggeratedly polite and the rule behind it is not.
= 那块牌子客气得过分，它背后那条规矩可不客气。
exaggeratedly polite = 客气得过分
构词：exaggerated 加 -ly

exaggeration
核心：夸张、夸大的说法：把事情说得过头，也指那句过头的话。可数不可数都行。
习语 it is no exaggeration to say… 是「说…并不为过」。
① It is no exaggeration to say the whole street heard it.
= 说整条街都听见了，并不为过。
it is no exaggeration to say = 说…并不为过
② The account is accurate once you take out two exaggerations.
= 去掉两处夸张，那份叙述就是准的。
an exaggeration = 一处夸大

exalt
核心：两支。一是（正式）褒扬、把…捧高；二是（正式）提升（地位、职衔）。
名词 exaltation，形容词 exalted。
① The book exalts one designer and forgets the four who built it.
= 那本书把一位设计师捧上天，忘了动手造它的那四个人。
exalt somebody = 褒扬、抬高某人
② He was exalted to a position that had no budget at all.
= 他被提到一个完全没有预算的位子上。
be exalted = 被提升

exaltation
核心：两支。一是（正式）狂喜、振奋：那种昂扬的情绪；二是抬举、提升这件事。不可数。
① The exaltation lasted until somebody read the second page.
= 那股振奋一直持续到有人读了第二页。
exaltation = 狂喜、振奋
② The exaltation of one name buried nine others.
= 一个名字被抬高，埋掉了另外九个。
the exaltation of somebody = 对某人的抬举

exalted
核心：两支。一是（地位）崇高的、显赫的；二是（情绪）昂扬的、兴奋的。
① From that exalted position he could see nothing that mattered.
= 在那个显赫的位置上，他什么要紧事也看不见。
an exalted position = 显赫的位置
② The mood was exalted for an evening and businesslike by Monday.
= 那份昂扬的情绪维持了一晚，到周一就公事公办了。
be exalted = 情绪昂扬

exam
核心：考试：examination 的日常说法，学校与资格考试都用。可数。
① The exam lasts three hours and the last question is worth a third.
= 那场考试三个钟头，最后一题占三分之一的分。
an exam = 考试
take an exam = 参加考试
pass an exam = 考试及格
② She failed the exam twice and teaches the subject now.
= 那门考试她挂过两回，如今在教这门课。
fail an exam = 考试不及格

examination
核心：三支。一是（正式）考试；二是检查、审查；三是（医学）体格检查。
可数不可数都行。习语 on closer examination 是细看之下。
① The examination is set by one board and marked by another.
= 那场考试由一个考试局出题，另一个批改。
an examination = 考试
② On closer examination the signature is in a different ink.
= 细看之下，那个签名用的是另一种墨水。
on closer examination = 细看之下
close examination = 仔细的查验
③ The examination took ten minutes and the waiting took two hours.
= 那次体检十分钟，等了两个钟头。
a medical examination = 体格检查

examine
核心：三支。一是仔细查看、审查；二是（医学）给…做检查；
三是（正式）考核、对…进行考试。名词 examination，人有 examiner 与 examinee。
① They examined the accounts for three weeks and found one wrong column.
= 他们查了三个星期的账，找出一栏是错的。
examine something = 仔细查看某物
examine the evidence = 审查证据
② The doctor examined her knee and asked about the other one.
= 医生检查了她的膝盖，问的却是另一条腿。
examine a patient = 给病人做检查

examinee
核心：应试者、考生：参加考试的人，跟 examiner 相对。可数，正式。
① Every examinee gets a number and loses a name for three hours.
= 每个考生领一个编号，三个钟头里没有名字。
an examinee = 应试者
② Examinees are seated in number order and separated by an arm's length.
= 考生按编号就座，彼此隔一臂的距离。
examinees = 考生（复数）
构词：examine 加 -ee

examiner
核心：两支。一是主考人、阅卷人：出题或评分的人；二是（正式）检查员、审查员。可数。
① The examiner marks four hundred scripts and remembers three of them.
= 那位阅卷人批四百份卷子，记得住三份。
an examiner = 主考人、阅卷人
② An external examiner reads a sample and signs the whole cohort off.
= 校外考官抽读一部分，签字放行整届学生。
an external examiner = 校外考官

example
核心：三支。一是例子、实例；二是榜样、典范；三是儆戒的例子。可数。
习语：for example 是例如；set an example 是树立榜样；
make an example of somebody 是拿某人开刀儆戒。
① Give one example and the room will argue about it for an hour.
= 举一个例子，屋里的人能就它争一个钟头。
an example = 例子
for example = 例如
② She sets an example by doing the job nobody signed up for.
= 她去做那件没人报名的活儿，以身作则。
set an example = 树立榜样
follow somebody's example = 效法某人
③ They made an example of one supplier and the rest paid on time.
= 他们拿一家供应商开刀儆戒，其余的都按时付了款。
make an example of somebody = 拿某人做儆戒

exasperate
核心：使恼火、把人惹得没脾气：说反复的小麻烦磨得人光火。
名词 exasperation，形容词 exasperated 与 exasperating。
注意别混：exasperate 是把人惹恼，exacerbate 是让事情变糟。
① The forms exasperate everybody and the office blames the software.
= 那些表格把人人惹得冒火，办公室怪软件。
exasperate somebody = 把某人惹恼
② What exasperates her is being asked the same thing in three emails.
= 让她光火的，是同一件事被三封邮件问三遍。
exasperate = 使恼火

exasperated
核心：恼火的、被磨得没脾气的：be exasperated by something、
be exasperated with somebody。
① She was exasperated by the third form and polite on all three.
= 第三张表把她惹恼了，三张表她都填得客客气气。
be exasperated by something = 被某事惹恼
② An exasperated parent counts to ten and gets to four.
= 一位快没脾气的家长数到十，其实数到四就爆了。
an exasperated sigh = 一声无奈的叹息
副词 exasperatedly：恼火地、没好气地：修饰说话、叹气、摆手的方式。
③ He shrugged exasperatedly and answered the question anyway.
= 他没好气地耸了耸肩，还是把问题答了。
shrug exasperatedly = 没好气地耸肩
④ She repeated the instruction exasperatedly and wrote it on the wall.
= 她恼火地又说了一遍那条说明，索性写到了墙上。
say something exasperatedly = 没好气地说
构词：exasperated 加 -ly

exasperating
核心：（事情）让人光火的、磨人的：说反复出小毛病的东西或做法。
① The lock is exasperating in winter and perfectly normal in June.
= 那把锁冬天磨人得很，到六月就好端端的。
exasperating = 让人光火的
② It is exasperating to explain the same rule to the same person.
= 同一条规矩对同一个人讲了又讲，实在磨人。
be exasperating = 令人恼火

exasperation
核心：恼火、无奈：被反复磨得没脾气的那股情绪。不可数。
① Her exasperation showed in the punctuation and not in the words.
= 她的恼火显在标点上，没显在措辞上。
exasperation = 恼火、无奈
in exasperation = 恼火之下
② In exasperation he took the door off its hinges.
= 一气之下，他把门从铰链上卸了下来。
a note of exasperation = 一丝无奈的口气

excavate
核心：两支。一是挖掘、开挖（土方）：excavate a trench；
二是（考古）发掘：把埋着的遗迹清理出来。名词 excavation。
① They excavated the site in one week and stopped for nine months.
= 他们一周就把场地挖开，然后停了九个月。
excavate a site = 开挖场地
② The tomb was excavated in 1923 and catalogued in 1978.
= 那座墓一九二三年发掘，一九七八年才编目。
excavate a tomb = 发掘一座墓

excavation
核心：两支。一是挖掘、开挖（这件事，也指挖出的坑）；
二是（考古）发掘工作。可数不可数都行。
① The excavation filled with water twice before the pump arrived.
= 泵还没到，那个基坑就灌了两回水。
an excavation = 挖开的坑
② Excavation of the site is licensed for three summers.
= 那个遗址的发掘获准做三个夏天。
the excavation of a site = 遗址的发掘

excavator
核心：两支。一是挖掘机：带铲斗的工程机械；二是（考古）发掘者。可数。
① The excavator took out in a day what nine people had planned for a week.
= 九个人计划干一周的活，挖掘机一天就挖完了。
an excavator = 挖掘机
② The excavator kept a notebook that is now worth more than the finds.
= 那位发掘者留下的笔记本，如今比出土物还值钱。
the original excavator = 最初的发掘者
构词：excavate 加 -or

exceed
核心：两支。一是超过（数量、限度）：exceed the limit；
二是超出（预期、职权）：exceed expectations、exceed your authority。
副词 exceedingly。
① The final bill exceeds the builder's estimate by about a third.
= 最后那张账单，比施工方的估价高出大约三分之一。
exceed something = 超过某数
exceed the speed limit = 超速
② The result exceeded every expectation and nobody had written those expectations down.
= 那个结果超出了所有预期，可那些预期谁也没白纸黑字写下来过。
exceed expectations = 超出预期

exceedingly
核心：（略正式）极其、非常：多修饰形容词，语气比 very 重。
① The instructions are exceedingly clear and the machine ignores them.
= 那份说明极其清楚，机器不理它。
exceedingly clear = 极其清楚
② It is exceedingly rare and the museum has four of them.
= 那东西极为罕见，那家博物馆有四件。
exceedingly rare = 极为罕见
构词：exceeding 加 -ly

excel
核心：擅长、出类拔萃：在某方面比别人强，搭 excel at 或 excel in something。
过去式 excelled，双写 l。名词 excellence。
习语 excel yourself 是（英式）超常发挥、比平时还出色。
① She excels at the part of the job everybody else avoids.
= 那份工作里别人都躲的部分，她做得最好。
excel at something = 在某方面出色
excel in something = 擅长某事
② He excelled himself and burnt only one of the four pans.
= 他超常发挥，四口锅里只烧糊了一口。
excel yourself = 超常发挥

excellence
核心：卓越、优秀：做得极好的那种水准。不可数。
习语 par excellence 是「典型的、首屈一指的」，放在名词后面。
① Excellence in that trade takes a decade and one good teacher.
= 那一行要做到出色，得十年，外加一位好师傅。
excellence = 卓越
academic excellence = 学业上的优异
② He is the committee man par excellence and has never chaired one.
= 他是典型的委员会型人物，却从没当过主席。
par excellence = 首屈一指的

Excellency
核心：阁下：对大使、总督一类高官的尊称，
当面说 Your Excellency，提到时说 His Excellency 或 Her Excellency。可数。
① The note begins Your Excellency and goes on to ask for a parking permit.
= 那张便条以「阁下」开头，接下去要的是一张停车许可。
Your Excellency = 阁下（当面称呼）
② His Excellency arrived late and left before the toast.
= 那位阁下来得晚，祝酒之前就走了。
His Excellency = 某某阁下

excellent
核心：极好的、优秀的：比 good 强得多；也作感叹表示赞许。副词 excellently。
① The food is excellent and the service is a matter of luck.
= 那儿的菜极好，服务全看运气。
excellent = 极好的
an excellent idea = 极好的主意
② 'They said yes.' 'Excellent.'
= 「他们答应了。」「太好了。」
excellent = 好极了（表示赞许）
副词 excellently：出色地、极好地：修饰做事的水准。
③ The piece was played excellently and programmed badly.
= 那首曲子演得极好，编排得很糟。
be played excellently = 演奏得极出色
④ She writes excellently about things she has not done.
= 她写自己没做过的事，写得极好。
write excellently = 写得出色
构词：excellent 加 -ly

except
核心：介词、连词与动词。介词是除…之外：everyone except him；
连词是「只是、要不是」：I would go, except it rains；
动词（正式）是把…除外：present company excepted。
注意别混：except 是「除…之外（不包括）」，besides 多是「除…之外（还有）」。
① Everyone except the caretaker had a key and he had all of them.
= 除了看门的，人人都有一把钥匙，而他手里全部都有。
except = 除…之外
except for something = 除某物之外
② I would walk, except that the last stretch has no pavement.
= 我本想走过去，只是最后那段没有人行道。
except that... = 只是…
③ Present company excepted, nobody reads the appendix.
= 在座各位除外，没人读附录。
be excepted = 被除外

excepting
核心：（正式）除…以外：介词，多用在 not excepting 与 always excepting 里，
日常用 except。
① Every window was painted shut, not excepting the one over the sink.
= 每扇窗都被漆死了，水槽上头那扇也不例外。
not excepting something = 连某物也不例外
② All the shops open on Sunday, always excepting the butcher.
= 所有店周日都开，肉铺永远除外。
excepting = 除…以外

exception
核心：两支。一是例外：不适用一般规则的人或事；
二是异议：take exception to something 是对某事表示不满。可数。
习语：with the exception of 是除…之外；without exception 是无一例外。
① There is one exception and it is written in a footnote.
= 有一个例外，写在脚注里。
an exception = 例外
make an exception = 破一次例
② With the exception of one afternoon, it rained all week.
= 除了一个下午，那一周都在下雨。
with the exception of something = 除某物之外
without exception = 无一例外
③ She took exception to the word and not to the decision.
= 她不满的是那个词，不是那个决定。
take exception to something = 对某事表示不满

exceptional
核心：两支。一是（水准）出众的、卓越的；二是例外的、不寻常的：
in exceptional circumstances 是在特殊情况下。副词 exceptionally。
① An exceptional year for apples means a bad year for the branches.
= 苹果特别好的年份，对树枝来说是难熬的年份。
exceptional = 出众的、异乎寻常的
② Leave is granted in exceptional circumstances and defined nowhere.
= 特殊情况下才准假，可什么算特殊哪儿也没写。
exceptional circumstances = 特殊情况
副词 exceptionally：两支。一是格外地、异常地：exceptionally cold； 二是例外地、破例地：exceptionally, we can wait。
③ It was an exceptionally cold March and the pipes agreed.
= 那年三月格外冷，水管也这么认为。
exceptionally cold = 格外冷
④ Exceptionally, the deadline was moved and moved back the same day.
= 那次破例把截止日期挪了，当天又挪了回来。
exceptionally = 破例地
构词：exceptional 加 -ly

excerpt
核心：节选、摘录：从书、演讲或乐曲里取出的一段。可数；
也作动词（多用被动）：be excerpted from something。
① The excerpt runs for two minutes and gives away the ending.
= 那段节选放两分钟，把结局给漏了。
an excerpt = 节选
an excerpt from something = 从某处摘出的一段
② The passage was excerpted in a magazine and read by more people than the book.
= 那一段被杂志摘登，读它的人比读那本书的还多。
be excerpted from something = 摘自某处

excess
核心：三支。一是过量、过度：不可数，to excess 是过度地；
二是超出的部分：an excess of something；
三是（英式保险）免赔额：the excess on a policy。也作定语：excess baggage。
① Excess salt is the reason that soup tastes of one thing.
= 盐放多了，那锅汤就只剩一个味道。
excess = 过量
an excess of something = 过多的某物
② He does everything to excess and sleeps four hours a night.
= 他干什么都过头，一晚睡四个钟头。
to excess = 过度地
③ The excess on that policy is more than the repair.
= 那份保单的免赔额，比修车还贵。
the excess = 免赔额
excess baggage = 超重行李

excessive
核心：过多的、过分的：说数量、程度或做法超出该有的限度。副词 excessively。
① The charge is excessive and the letter explaining it is longer than the bill.
= 那笔收费过高，解释它的信比账单还长。
excessive = 过多的、过分的
excessive force = 过度的武力
② Excessive noise is defined in decibels and argued about in person.
= 噪音过量是用分贝定的，争起来却是当面吵。
excessive noise = 过量的噪音
副词 excessively：过分地、过度地：修饰形容词与动词。
③ The room is excessively warm and the window opens two centimetres.
= 那屋子热得过分，窗户只开得了两厘米。
excessively warm = 热得过分
④ He apologised excessively and repeated the mistake on Thursday.
= 他道歉道得过了头，周四又犯了同样的错。
apologise excessively = 过分地道歉
构词：excessive 加 -ly

exchange
核心：名词四支、动词两支。名词一是交换、互换：an exchange of letters；
二是（简短的）交谈、争执；三是交易所：the stock exchange；
四是（学生的）交流项目。动词一是交换：exchange something for something；
二是互换（意见、眼神）。
习语 in exchange for something 是作为交换。
① The exchange of contracts took nine weeks and one afternoon of panic.
= 交换合同花了九个星期，外加一个下午的手忙脚乱。
an exchange = 交换
an exchange of letters = 往来书信
② She exchanged the shirt for one two sizes larger.
= 她把那件衬衫换成了大两号的。
exchange something for something = 把某物换成某物
③ They took the studio in exchange for two paintings a year.
= 他们拿到那间画室，作为交换，每年给两幅画。
in exchange for something = 作为对某物的交换
④ The stock exchange closed early and the news arrived later.
= 交易所提前收市，消息来得更晚。
the stock exchange = 证券交易所

exchangeable
核心：可交换的、可调换的：说票、券、商品能换成别的或退换。
① The ticket is exchangeable once and refundable never.
= 那张票能换一次，退是不能退的。
be exchangeable = 可以调换
② Vouchers are exchangeable for goods and not for money.
= 那些代金券能换东西，不能换钱。
exchangeable for something = 可换成某物
构词：exchange 加 -able

exchequer
核心：（英国）国库、财政部：the Exchequer 指国家财政，
the Chancellor of the Exchequer 是财政大臣。单数，常带 the。
① The Exchequer collects it in April and spends it in the same week.
= 国库四月把钱收上来，同一周就花出去。
the Exchequer = 国库、财政部
② A windfall for the Exchequer is a bill for somebody else.
= 国库的一笔意外之财，是另一头的一张账单。
the Chancellor of the Exchequer = 英国财政大臣

excise
核心：名词与动词，重音不同。名词是消费税：对国内生产的烟、酒、燃油等征的税；
动词一是（外科）切除；二是（正式）删掉（文字段落）。名词还有 excision。
① Excise on spirits changed twice and the price changed three times.
= 烈酒的消费税调了两回，价钱调了三回。
excise duty = 消费税
excise = 消费税
② The surgeon excised the lump and sent it away in a pot.
= 外科医生把那个肿块切了下来，装罐送去化验。
excise something = 切除某物
③ Two paragraphs were excised and the argument still limps.
= 有两段被删掉了，论证还是一瘸一拐。
be excised = 被删掉

excision
核心：两支。一是（外科）切除：把病变组织割掉；
二是（正式）删除：把文字段落去掉。可数不可数都行。
① The excision was clean and the report came back in nine days.
= 那次切除很干净，报告九天后回来。
an excision = 一次切除
② Excision of that chapter improved the book and annoyed the author.
= 删掉那一章让书更好，也惹恼了作者。
the excision of something = 对某部分的删除

excitability
核心：两支。一是易激动、性子急：说人一点就着；
二是（生理）兴奋性：神经或肌肉受刺激的反应能力。不可数。
① His excitability is an asset on stage and a problem in meetings.
= 他那份易激动，在台上是优点，在会上是麻烦。
excitability = 易激动
② Nerve excitability rises with a fever and falls with the drug.
= 发烧时神经兴奋性升高，用药之后降下来。
nerve excitability = 神经兴奋性

excitable
核心：易激动的、一点就着的：说人或动物容易兴奋起来。名词 excitability。
① An excitable dog and a quiet street are a poor match.
= 一条容易激动的狗，配一条安静的街，不是好搭配。
an excitable dog = 容易激动的狗
② She is excitable about small news and calm about large.
= 小消息能让她激动，大事她倒沉得住气。
be excitable = 易激动的

excite
核心：三支。一是使兴奋、使激动：excite somebody；
二是（正式）引起（兴趣、怀疑、议论）：excite comment；
三是（物理、生理）激发（粒子、神经）。名词 excitement。
① The plan excites everyone under thirty and worries the accountant.
= 那个计划让三十岁以下的人都兴奋，让会计发愁。
excite somebody = 使某人兴奋
② The delay excited comment and then excited a formal complaint.
= 那次延误先是引来议论，接着引来一封正式投诉。
excite comment = 引起议论
③ The laser excites the atoms and the light comes out one colour.
= 激光把原子激发起来，出来的光只有一个颜色。
excite atoms = 激发原子

excited
核心：兴奋的、激动的：be excited about something、be excited to do something。
副词 excitedly。
注意别混：excited 是人感到兴奋，exciting 是事物让人兴奋。
① The children were excited about the coach and bored by the castle.
= 孩子们为那辆大巴兴奋，对城堡兴味索然。
be excited about something = 为某事兴奋
get excited = 激动起来
② He was too excited to eat and finished everybody else's chips.
= 他兴奋得吃不下饭，把别人的薯条全吃了。
be excited to do something = 兴奋地要去做某事
副词 excitedly：兴奋地、激动地：修饰说、跑、指点的样子。
③ She talked excitedly for ten minutes and forgot to say where.
= 她兴奋地说了十分钟，忘了说在哪儿。
talk excitedly = 兴奋地说
④ The dog ran excitedly to the door and barked at the wrong person.
= 那狗兴奋地跑到门口，冲着不相干的人叫。
run excitedly = 兴奋地跑
构词：excited 加 -ly

excitement
核心：两支。一是兴奋、激动（这种情绪），不可数；
二是（一件）令人兴奋的事，可数。
① The excitement lasted until the queue started moving slowly.
= 那股兴奋，一直持续到队伍开始挪得很慢。
excitement = 兴奋
in excitement = 兴奋之下
② The excitements of that month are a photograph and a receipt.
= 那个月的兴奋事，如今只剩一张照片和一张收据。
an excitement = 一件令人兴奋的事

exciting
核心：令人兴奋的、精彩的：说事物本身让人激动。副词 excitingly。
① The first half was exciting and the second half was a lesson.
= 上半场精彩，下半场是一堂课。
exciting = 令人兴奋的
an exciting match = 精彩的比赛
② The exciting part is the first week and the work is all the rest.
= 最带劲的是头一个星期，其余全是干活。
the exciting part = 最带劲的那部分
an exciting prospect = 令人期待的前景
副词 excitingly：令人兴奋地、精彩地：修饰做法、写法与安排。
③ The programme is excitingly planned and impossible to follow.
= 那个节目安排得让人兴奋，就是跟不上。
be excitingly planned = 安排得令人兴奋
④ More excitingly, the second box turned out to be full.
= 更让人兴奋的是，第二个箱子居然是满的。
more excitingly = 更令人兴奋的是
构词：exciting 加 -ly

exclaim
核心：（正式或书面）惊叫、大声说：因惊讶、喜悦或愤怒脱口而出。
名词 exclamation。
① 'Not that one!' she exclaimed, and everybody reached for that one.
= 「别拿那个！」她叫道，结果人人都去拿那个。
exclaim = 惊叫、脱口而出
② He exclaimed at the price and paid it without further comment.
= 他见了价钱惊叫一声，二话没说就付了。
exclaim at something = 对某事惊叫

exclamation
核心：两支。一是惊叫、感叹（的话）；
二是感叹号：an exclamation mark（美式 exclamation point）。可数。
① The exclamation was in Welsh and the meaning was clear to everyone.
= 那声惊叫是威尔士语，意思人人都懂。
an exclamation = 一声惊叫
give an exclamation = 发出一声惊叫
② Three exclamation marks in one email undo the sentence they end.
= 一封邮件里三个感叹号，把它们收尾的那句话毁了。
an exclamation mark = 感叹号

exclamatory
核心：（语法或文体）感叹的：说句子或语气带惊叹意味。
① An exclamatory sentence needs no question and gets an answer anyway.
= 感叹句本不发问，照样有人来答。
an exclamatory sentence = 感叹句
② The style is exclamatory and the facts are three sentences long.
= 那种文风满是惊叹，事实只有三句话。
exclamatory = 感叹的
构词：exclamation 加 -ory

exclude
核心：三支。一是把…排除在外、不包括：exclude somebody from something；
二是排除（可能性）：exclude the possibility；三是（英国学校）勒令停学。
名词 exclusion，跟 include 相对。
① The price excludes delivery and the delivery costs more than the chair.
= 那个价钱不含运费，运费比椅子还贵。
exclude something = 不包括某物
exclude somebody from something = 把某人排除在某事之外
② The report excludes the possibility of a leak and names no source.
= 那份报告排除了泄漏的可能，却没点出依据。
exclude the possibility = 排除可能性

excluding
核心：不包括、除…之外：介词，多用于价目与统计 ——
excluding VAT 是不含增值税。跟 including 相对。
① The total is nine hundred, excluding the fee for the survey.
= 总数是九百，不含勘验费。
excluding something = 不包括某物
excluding VAT = 不含增值税
② Everyone excluding the two on leave was in the room.
= 除了休假的那两位，所有人都在屋里。
excluding = 除…之外

exclusion
核心：两支。一是排除在外、把人拒之门外；
二是（保险、合同的）除外条款：the exclusions on a policy。可数不可数都行。
习语 to the exclusion of something 是「到了把别的都撇开的地步」。
① Exclusion from that list costs a supplier half a year's work.
= 被排除在那份名单外，供应商就少了半年的活。
exclusion = 排除在外
exclusion from something = 被排除在某事之外
② He works on one problem to the exclusion of everything else.
= 他只做一个问题，别的一概撇开。
to the exclusion of something = 撇开其他一切
③ The exclusions are printed on the back and matter more than the cover.
= 那些除外条款印在背面，比封面上的话要紧。
an exclusion = 除外条款
④ The court made an exclusion order and the police drew a two-mile exclusion zone round the site.
= 法庭下了禁入令，警方在场地周围划出两英里的禁区。
an exclusion order = 禁入令
an exclusion zone = 禁区

exclusionary
核心：（正式）排他的、把人挡在外面的：说规则或做法有意无意地排斥某些人。
① The rule is exclusionary in effect and neutral in wording.
= 那条规矩措辞中立，效果上却是排他的。
exclusionary = 排他的
② Exclusionary practices leave no paperwork and a long queue outside.
= 排斥性的做法不留文件，只在门外留下长队。
exclusionary practices = 排斥性的做法

exclusive
核心：四支。一是独有的、专属的：exclusive rights；
二是（场所、圈子）高档排外的：an exclusive club；
三是互斥的：mutually exclusive；四是名词「独家报道」。副词 exclusively。
① The exclusive rights lasted two years and were sold on twice.
= 那份独家权利有效两年，中途转卖过两回。
exclusive rights = 独家权利
an exclusive contract = 独家合同
② The two explanations are not mutually exclusive and both are partly right.
= 那两种解释并不互相排斥，两个都对了一半。
mutually exclusive = 互相排斥的
③ The paper ran the exclusive on a Sunday and everyone had it by Monday.
= 那份报纸周日登出独家，到周一人人都有了。
an exclusive = 独家报道
④ The rent is nine hundred a month exclusive of bills.
= 房租一个月九百，不含各项费用。
exclusive of something = 不包括某物
副词 exclusively：仅仅、专门地：只针对某一群人或某一件事。
⑤ The club is exclusively for members and the bar is not.
= 那家俱乐部只对会员开放，酒吧不是。
exclusively = 仅仅、专门地
⑥ She works exclusively in pencil and sells everything she draws.
= 她只用铅笔画，画什么卖什么。
work exclusively on something = 只做某件事
构词：exclusive 加 -ly

exclusiveness
核心：排他性、门槛感：一个团体或场所把外人挡在外面的那种性质。不可数。
① The exclusiveness of the club is the product and the wine is not.
= 那家俱乐部卖的是那份门槛感，不是酒。
exclusiveness = 排他性
② Exclusiveness costs nothing to advertise and everything to keep up.
= 门槛感宣传起来不花钱，维持起来什么都得花。
the exclusiveness of something = 某处的排他性
构词：exclusive 加 -ness

exclusivity
核心：两支。一是独家性、专有权：只此一家的地位；
二是排他性、门槛感，跟 exclusiveness 同义。不可数。
① Exclusivity is written into the contract for eighteen months.
= 合同里写明独家权利十八个月。
exclusivity = 独家性
② The shop trades on exclusivity and opens four hours a day.
= 那家店做的是稀缺的生意，一天只开四个钟头。
an exclusivity clause = 独家条款

excommunicate
核心：（宗教）把…逐出教会：正式剥夺某人领受圣事与教会成员的资格。
名词 excommunication。
① He was excommunicated in one sentence and buried in the same churchyard.
= 他被一句话逐出教会，最后葬在同一片教堂墓地。
excommunicate somebody = 把某人逐出教会
② Councils excommunicated kings and kings ignored two of them.
= 宗教会议开除过国王的教籍，其中两次国王根本不理。
be excommunicated = 被逐出教会

excommunication
核心：（宗教）绝罚、逐出教会：把人开除教籍的处分。可数不可数都行。
① Excommunication was a political instrument long before it was a mercy.
= 逐出教会长期是政治工具，之后才谈得上是怜悯。
excommunication = 逐出教会
② The excommunication was lifted three centuries after the man died.
= 那道绝罚，在当事人死后三百年才解除。
lift an excommunication = 解除绝罚
构词：excommunicate 加 -ion

excoriate
核心：两支。一是（正式）严厉抨击：把人或做法批得体无完肤；
二是（医学）擦伤、剥脱（皮肤）。名词 excoriation。
① The review excoriates the design and praises the paper it is printed on.
= 那篇评论把设计批得一无是处，倒夸了印它的纸。
excoriate somebody = 严厉抨击某人
② The strap excoriated the skin and the sore took a month.
= 那条带子把皮肤磨破了，疮口好了一个月。
excoriate the skin = 磨破皮肤

excoriation
核心：两支。一是（正式）严厉抨击；二是（医学）表皮擦伤、剥脱处。可数不可数都行。
① The excoriation in that editorial was aimed at one committee.
= 那篇社论里的猛烈抨击，冲的是一个委员会。
excoriation = 严厉抨击
② Excoriation around the wound is what the dressing is for.
= 伤口周围的表皮擦伤，正是那层敷料要管的。
an excoriation = 一处表皮擦伤

excrement
核心：（正式）粪便：人或动物排出的固体废物。不可数。
日常说 poo（英式儿语）或 faeces（医学）。
① Excrement in the water supply is what that whole report is about.
= 那整份报告讲的，就是饮用水里的粪便污染。
excrement = 粪便
human excrement = 人的粪便
② Bird excrement takes the paint off a car in one summer.
= 鸟粪一个夏天就能把车漆蚀掉。
bird excrement = 鸟粪

excremental
核心：（正式）粪便的、跟排泄物有关的：也用来骂东西糟得不堪。
① The excremental smell led them to a pipe under the floor.
= 那股粪便味把他们引到地板下的一根管子。
an excremental smell = 粪便的臭味
② His review is excremental in tone and precise in detail.
= 他那篇评论口气极难听，细节倒很准确。
excremental = 粪便的、污秽的
构词：excrement 加 -al

excrescence
核心：两支。一是（医学、生物）赘生物：长在体表多出来的一块；
二是（比喻，贬）多余而难看的东西：说建筑或装饰不该有。可数。
① The excrescence on the trunk is a gall and the tree is fine.
= 树干上那个瘤是虫瘿，树本身没事。
an excrescence = 赘生物
② The porch is an excrescence and the rest of the house is plain.
= 那个门廊是个多余的累赘，房子其余部分朴素得很。
an architectural excrescence = 建筑上多余难看的部分

excreta
核心：（正式或医学）排泄物：粪、尿与汗的统称。作复数看待，不可数用法也有。
① Excreta from the pens are collected daily and spread in autumn.
= 圈里的排泄物每天清出来，秋天撒到地里。
excreta = 排泄物
② Excreta are analysed for what the animal could not digest.
= 化验排泄物，看的是那头牲口没消化掉什么。
animal excreta = 动物排泄物

excrete
核心：（生物）排泄、排出：把废物排出体外，跟 secrete 分泌相对。名词 excretion。
① The kidneys excrete what the liver has already broken down.
= 肾脏排出的，是肝脏已经分解过的东西。
excrete something = 排出某物
② Plants excrete through the leaves and nobody watches them do it.
= 植物通过叶片排出废物，没人看着它们排。
excrete waste = 排出废物

excretion
核心：两支。一是排泄（这个过程）；二是排泄物。可数不可数都行。
① Excretion is the part of the diagram that the class always skips.
= 那张图上排泄那一环，班上总被跳过去。
excretion = 排泄
② The excretions are collected in a bag and measured twice a day.
= 那些排出物收在袋子里，一天量两次。
an excretion = 排泄物
构词：excrete 加 -ion

excretory
核心：（生物）排泄的：说负责排出废物的器官与系统。只作定语。
① The excretory system does its work and gets one paragraph.
= 排泄系统干着自己的活，课本只给它一段。
the excretory system = 排泄系统
② Excretory organs differ across species and the job does not.
= 各物种的排泄器官不同，要干的活是一样的。
excretory organs = 排泄器官
构词：excrete 加 -ory

excruciating
核心：两支。一是（疼痛）剧烈难忍的：excruciating pain；
二是（场面）尴尬难熬的：an excruciating silence。副词 excruciatingly。
① The pain was excruciating for two minutes and gone by the third.
= 那阵疼有两分钟难忍到极点，到第三分钟就没了。
excruciating pain = 剧痛
② The speech was excruciating and the applause was polite.
= 那篇讲话让人如坐针毡，掌声倒是客气。
excruciating = 令人难堪难熬的
副词 excruciatingly：难忍地、难堪地：修饰形容词，说程度到了受不了的地步。
③ The meeting was excruciatingly slow and finished four minutes early.
= 那个会慢得让人受不了，倒提前四分钟散了。
excruciatingly slow = 慢得难忍
④ She was excruciatingly polite to a man who had lost her luggage.
= 对一个把她行李弄丢的人，她客气得让人替她难受。
excruciatingly polite = 客气得让人难受
构词：excruciating 加 -ly

exculpate
核心：（正式、法律）开脱、证明无罪：说证据或说法洗清某人的责任，
跟 inculpate 使人涉罪相对。名词 exculpation。
① The tape exculpates one driver and implicates the other.
= 那段录像洗清了一位司机，牵连了另一位。
exculpate somebody = 为某人开脱罪责
② Nothing in the file exculpates him and nothing convicts him either.
= 卷宗里既没有为他开脱的东西，也没有能定他罪的。
exculpate somebody from something = 免除某人在某事上的罪责

exculpation
核心：（正式、法律）开脱、洗脱罪责：证明某人无责这件事。不可数。
① Exculpation came nine years late and in two paragraphs.
= 洗脱罪名来得晚了九年，只有两段话。
exculpation = 开脱罪责
② The letter reads as exculpation and was written as an apology.
= 那封信读着像在开脱，写的时候是想道歉。
exculpation of somebody = 对某人的开脱

excursion
核心：两支。一是短途旅行、远足：安排好的一次出游；
二是（比喻）涉足：an excursion into something 是跨行一试。可数。
① The excursion leaves at eight and returns when the driver decides.
= 那趟远足八点出发，几点回来由司机定。
an excursion = 短途旅行
go on an excursion = 去远足
② His excursion into politics lasted one term and two arguments.
= 他涉足政界，维持了一个任期和两场争吵。
an excursion into something = 涉足某个领域

excusable
核心：情有可原的、可以原谅的：说错误或行为在当时情形下说得过去。
跟 inexcusable 不可原谅的相对。
① A first mistake is excusable and the third one is a pattern.
= 头一回犯错情有可原，第三回就是习惯了。
excusable = 情有可原的
an excusable error = 可以原谅的错误
② Lateness is excusable in that weather and not in that car.
= 那种天气里迟到情有可原，开那种车就说不过去。
be excusable = 可以原谅

excuse
核心：名词与动词，读音不同。名词是借口、理由：make an excuse。
动词一是原谅：excuse somebody for something；二是免除、准许离开：
be excused from something。
习语：Excuse me 是（引起注意、道歉或借过）劳驾、对不起；
make excuses 是找借口。
① The excuse was the traffic and the traffic that morning was moving perfectly.
= 借口是堵车，可那天早上的车流一路顺畅。
an excuse = 借口
make an excuse = 找个借口
② Excuse me — is this seat taken or is the coat travelling alone?
= 劳驾，这位子有人吗，还是那件外套自己在坐车？
excuse me = 劳驾、对不起
③ She excused him for the first missed rehearsal and not for the second.
= 头一次缺席排练她原谅了他，第二次没有。
excuse somebody for something = 因某事原谅某人
④ Two of them were excused from the meeting and heard about it anyway.
= 有两个人获准不参加那个会，照样听说了会上的事。
be excused from something = 获准免于某事

exeat
核心：（英国学校与学院）准假外出：正式批准学生离校一段时间，也指那张假条。可数。
① The exeat runs from Friday afternoon to Sunday night.
= 那次准假从周五下午到周日夜里。
an exeat = 准假外出
② Two boys came back from exeat with a rabbit and no explanation.
= 有两个男生请假回来带了只兔子，一句解释也没有。
be back from exeat = 请假外出后返校

exec
核心：（口语）高管、主管：executive 的简说，多用于商界与媒体。可数。
① The exec flew in for one meeting and left before the sandwiches.
= 那位高管为一个会飞过来，三明治还没上就走了。
an exec = 高管
② Two execs and one engineer decided what the product would do.
= 两位高管加一名工程师，定下了那个产品要做什么。
a senior exec = 资深高管

execrable
核心：（正式）糟透的、恶劣至极的：说品质、口味或行为差得离谱。
① The coffee is execrable and the queue is out of the door.
= 那咖啡糟透了，队还排到了门外。
execrable = 糟透的
② An execrable pun is still a pun and the room still groans.
= 再烂的双关也是双关，屋里照样一片哀嚎。
execrable taste = 糟糕透顶的品味

executable
核心：两支。一是（计算机）可执行文件：能直接运行的程序文件；
二是（正式）可实行的、能执行的。可数。
① The executable is four megabytes and does one thing.
= 那个可执行文件四兆，只干一件事。
an executable = 可执行文件
② A plan is only executable if somebody has the time to execute it.
= 一个计划要能执行，得有人有时间去执行它。
be executable = 可以执行的

execute
核心：三支。一是处决：依判决把人处死，多用被动；
二是执行、实施（计划、命令）；三是（正式）完成（动作、作品）。名词 execution。
① He was executed in the prison yard in 1789 and pardoned by a committee in 1997.
= 他一七八九年在监狱院子里被处决，一九九七年由一个委员会平反。
be executed = 被处决
② The rescue plan was well executed on the night and badly explained afterwards.
= 那套救援方案当晚执行得很好，事后解释得很糟。
execute a plan = 执行计划
③ She executed the turn perfectly and lost a point for a wobble on landing.
= 那个转体她做得完美，落地晃了一下扣掉一分。
execute a movement = 完成一个动作

execution
核心：三支。一是处决：依法处死；二是执行、实施：the execution of a plan；
三是（正式）（表演、作品的）完成水准。可数不可数都行。
① The execution was public and the crowd was smaller than expected.
= 那次处决是公开的，围观的人比预想的少。
an execution = 一次处决
② The idea is good and the execution decided everything.
= 主意不错，最后见分晓的是执行。
the execution of a plan = 计划的执行
③ The execution is flawless and the piece is dull.
= 演奏技巧无懈可击，曲子本身沉闷。
execution = 演奏或完成的水准

executioner
核心：行刑者、刽子手：执行死刑的人。可数。
① The executioner was paid by the job and lived outside the walls.
= 刽子手按件计酬，住在城墙外面。
an executioner = 行刑者
② A masked executioner is a stage picture and not a historical rule.
= 蒙面的刽子手是舞台形象，不是历史通例。
a masked executioner = 蒙面的行刑者
构词：execution 加 -er

executive
核心：三支。一是（企业的）高管、主管：a senior executive；
二是（组织的）执行机构、领导班子：the executive of a union；
三是（政府的）行政部门：跟立法与司法并列。也作形容词：executive powers。
① The executive who signed it left before the ink was dry.
= 签字那位高管，墨迹还没干就走了。
an executive = 高管
a chief executive = 首席执行官
② The executive meets on Tuesdays and the members hear about it in the newsletter.
= 领导班子周二开会，会员是从通讯里知道结果的。
the executive = 执行机构
③ Executive powers are checked by two other branches and one newspaper.
= 行政权力受另外两个分支制约，外加一份报纸。
executive powers = 行政权力
④ The executive director signs the accounts and the executive secretary keeps the diary.
= 执行董事在账目上签字，行政秘书管日程。
an executive director = 执行董事
an executive secretary = 行政秘书
⑤ The president signed an executive order in the morning and claimed executive privilege by Friday.
= 总统上午签了一道行政命令，到周五就主张起行政特权。
an executive order = 行政命令
executive privilege = 行政特权
⑥ Nobody read past the executive summary and the real number sat in the appendix.
= 谁也没往内容提要后面读，真正的数字在附录里。
an executive summary = 内容提要

executor
核心：（法律）遗嘱执行人：按遗嘱办理死者财产的人。可数，女性旧称 executrix。
① The executor spent two years on an estate worth very little.
= 那位遗嘱执行人为一笔不值几个钱的遗产忙了两年。
an executor = 遗嘱执行人
② Being named executor is an honour and about nine months of work.
= 被指定为遗嘱执行人是一份荣誉，也是大约九个月的活。
be appointed executor = 被指定为遗嘱执行人

exegesis
核心：（正式）释经、注疏：对文本尤其是经文的详细解释与分析。
可数不可数都行，复数 exegeses。
① The exegesis runs to three volumes and the text is four pages.
= 那部注疏写了三卷，原文只有四页。
exegesis = 释经、注疏
② Biblical exegesis is a trade with its own tools and its own quarrels.
= 圣经释义是一门手艺，有自己的工具，也有自己的争吵。
biblical exegesis = 圣经释义

exemplar
核心：（正式）典范、范例：拿来做标准或样板的人或物。可数。
① The building is an exemplar of a style that lasted nine years.
= 那栋楼是一种只流行了九年的风格的典范。
an exemplar = 典范
an exemplar of something = 某种事物的范例
② Teachers keep two exemplars and mark everything against them.
= 老师留两份范文，一切都照着它们批。
exemplars = 范例（复数）

exemplary
核心：三支。一是堪称楷模的、模范的：exemplary conduct；
二是（惩罚）以儆效尤的：an exemplary sentence；
三是（法律）惩罚性的：exemplary damages 是惩罚性赔偿。
① Her attendance is exemplary and her handwriting is a separate matter.
= 她的出勤堪称模范，字迹是另一回事。
exemplary conduct = 模范的举止
exemplary = 堪称楷模的
② The court imposed an exemplary sentence and the rest of the trade noticed.
= 法院判了一个以儆效尤的刑，同行都留意到了。
an exemplary sentence = 以儆效尤的判罚
exemplary damages = 惩罚性赔偿

exemplification
核心：（正式）例证、举例说明：拿实例把道理讲清楚这件事，也指那个实例。
可数不可数都行。
① The chapter is four pages of theory and one of exemplification.
= 那一章是四页理论加一页例证。
exemplification = 举例说明
② Exemplification of the rule takes longer than the rule itself.
= 给那条规则举例，比规则本身还费时间。
the exemplification of something = 对某规则的例证
构词：exemplify 的名词

exemplify
核心：两支。一是（正式）是…的典型、体现出：说某人某物最能代表某种特质；
二是举例说明：用例子把话讲清楚。过去式 exemplified。
① That building exemplifies everything the committee said it wanted.
= 那栋楼把委员会说想要的一切都体现了出来。
exemplify something = 典型地体现某物
② She exemplified the point with a receipt and stopped the argument.
= 她拿一张收据把那点讲明白，争论就停了。
exemplify a point = 举例说明一点

exempt
核心：形容词与动词。形容词是被免除的、豁免的：be exempt from something；
动词是免除、豁免：exempt somebody from something。名词 exemption。
① Under-fives are exempt from the charge and nobody checks the age.
= 五岁以下免收那笔费用，也没人查年龄。
be exempt from something = 免于某事
exempt = 获豁免的
② The act exempts one industry and names it in a footnote.
= 那部法令豁免了一个行业，把它写在脚注里。
exempt somebody from something = 免除某人的某项义务

exemption
核心：豁免、免除（这件事或那一项）：不必履行某种义务或缴纳某种费用。
可数不可数都行。
① The exemption applies for one year and is renewed by post.
= 那项豁免有效一年，靠寄信续办。
an exemption = 一项豁免
② Exemption from the test does not exempt anyone from the fee.
= 免考并不等于免费。
exemption from something = 对某事的豁免

exercise
核心：名词四支、动词三支。名词一是锻炼、运动，不可数；
二是（一次）练习、习题，可数；三是（军事）演习；四是（有目的的）活动、举措：
a public relations exercise 是一场公关操作。
动词一是锻炼；二是（正式）行使（权利、权力）：exercise your right；三是使（狗、马）活动。
习语 take exercise 是做运动。
① She takes exercise the way other people take medicine.
= 她做运动的样子，跟别人吃药一样。
take exercise = 做运动
exercise = 锻炼
② The exercise on page nine has an answer that the book gets wrong.
= 第九页那道练习，书上给的答案是错的。
an exercise = 一道练习
③ The whole review was a paper exercise and changed one form.
= 整场评估是纸面功夫，只改了一张表。
a paper exercise = 走形式的纸面功夫
④ Every citizen may exercise that right and about a third do.
= 每位公民都可以行使那项权利，实际行使的大约三分之一。
exercise a right = 行使权利
exercise caution = 谨慎行事
⑤ He exercises the dog before breakfast and himself afterwards.
= 他早饭前遛狗，早饭后才轮到自己。
exercise a dog = 遛狗
⑥ The spare room holds an exercise bike, an exercise ball and the ironing.
= 那间空房里放着一辆健身车、一个健身球，还有待熨的衣服。
an exercise bike = 健身车
an exercise ball = 健身球

exert
核心：两支。一是施加（力量、影响、压力）：exert pressure on somebody；
二是 exert yourself 是尽力、使劲。名词 exertion。
① The union exerted pressure for a year and settled for a review.
= 工会施压了一年，最后接受了一次评估了事。
exert pressure on somebody = 对某人施压
exert influence = 施加影响
② He exerted himself once in that job and it showed for a week.
= 那份工作里他使过一回劲，效果显了一个星期。
exert yourself = 使劲、尽力
exert oneself = 使劲、出力（同 exert yourself）

exertion
核心：两支。一是费力、用力：体力上的付出；
二是（正式）（权力、影响的）行使。可数不可数都行。
① Any exertion in that heat is a bad idea before four o'clock.
= 那种热天里，四点之前使力气都不明智。
exertion = 费力、用力
physical exertion = 体力消耗
② The exertion of influence is hard to prove and easy to feel.
= 影响力的行使很难证明，感觉倒明显。
the exertion of something = 对某种力量的行使

exeunt
核心：（戏剧舞台指示）众人下场：拉丁语，写在剧本里表示两个以上角色退场，
一个人下场用 exit。
① The scene ends with exeunt and one very slow door.
= 那场戏以众人退场收尾，外加一扇极慢的门。
exeunt = 众人下场
② Exeunt omnes means everybody leaves the stage at once.
= 那句舞台指示的意思是全体一齐退场。
exeunt omnes = 全体退场

exfoliate
核心：两支。一是（美容）去角质：把皮肤表面的死细胞搓掉；
二是（树皮、岩石）成片剥落。名词 exfoliation。
① She exfoliates twice a week and the label says daily.
= 她一周去两次角质，标签上写的是每天。
exfoliate = 去角质
② The bark exfoliates in strips and the trunk looks skinned.
= 树皮一条条剥落，树干看着像被剥了皮。
exfoliate = 成片剥落

exfoliation
核心：两支。一是（美容）去角质；二是（地质、植物）片状剥落。不可数。
① Exfoliation removes what soap leaves behind and takes ten seconds.
= 去角质去掉的是肥皂留下的那层，十秒钟就够。
exfoliation = 去角质
② Exfoliation of the rock face happens after frost and before rain.
= 那面岩壁的片状剥落，发生在霜后雨前。
the exfoliation of something = 某物的片状剥落
构词：exfoliate 加 -ion

exhalation
核心：（正式）呼气：把气吐出去这件事，也指吐出的那口气。可数不可数都行。
① Each exhalation lasts twice as long as the breath in.
= 每一次呼气，时长是吸气的两倍。
an exhalation = 一次呼气
② Exhalation carries out more water than most people expect.
= 呼气带走的水分，比多数人以为的多。
exhalation = 呼气
构词：exhale 的名词

exhale
核心：呼气、吐出：把气或烟从肺里送出去，跟 inhale 吸气相对。
① Exhale slowly and the count is easier to keep.
= 慢慢把气呼出去，数数就容易跟上。
exhale = 呼气
② He exhaled and the whole room realised it had been waiting.
= 他吐出一口气，整屋子人才发觉自己一直屏着。
exhale smoke = 吐出烟

exhaust
核心：三支。一是使筋疲力尽：多用被动 be exhausted；
二是用尽、耗光：exhaust the supply；三是名词：废气、排气管。
名词还有 exhaustion，形容词 exhausted 与 exhausting。
① Two nights of that would exhaust anybody and he did nine.
= 那样熬两夜谁都受不了，他熬了九夜。
exhaust somebody = 使某人筋疲力尽
② They exhausted the supply of chairs and started on the boxes.
= 椅子用光了，他们开始搬箱子当座。
exhaust something = 用尽某物
exhaust every possibility = 想尽一切办法
③ The exhaust is louder than the engine and legal by two decibels.
= 那根排气管比发动机还响，比法定上限低两分贝。
an exhaust = 排气管
exhaust fumes = 汽车废气
an exhaust pipe = 排气管

exhausted
核心：两支。一是筋疲力尽的：be exhausted by something；
二是（资源）耗尽的：an exhausted seam 是采空的矿层。
① She was exhausted by the journey and could not sleep on arrival.
= 那趟路把她累垮了，到了却睡不着。
be exhausted = 筋疲力尽
be exhausted by something = 被某事累垮
② The mine closed when the seam was exhausted and the village stayed.
= 矿层采空后矿关了，村子留了下来。
an exhausted seam = 采空的矿层

exhausting
核心：（事情）累人的、耗尽体力的：说活动或人让人筋疲力尽。
注意别混：exhausted 是人累坏了，exhausting 是事情累人。
① The journey is exhausting and the arrival makes up for it.
= 那趟路累人，到了之后觉得值。
exhausting = 累人的
an exhausting day = 累人的一天
② Being cheerful all day is exhausting and nobody counts it as work.
= 整天保持愉快是件累人的事，可没人把它算作工作。
be exhausting = 让人筋疲力尽

exhaustion
核心：两支。一是筋疲力尽、极度疲劳：也指医学上的衰竭；
二是（资源的）耗尽。不可数。
① He was treated for exhaustion and discharged the same evening.
= 他因体力衰竭接受治疗，当晚就出院了。
exhaustion = 筋疲力尽
nervous exhaustion = 神经衰竭
② Exhaustion of the fishery took thirty years and one new net.
= 那片渔场的枯竭用了三十年，外加一种新网。
the exhaustion of something = 某资源的耗尽

exhaustive
核心：详尽无遗的、彻底的：说调查、清单或检索把该查的都查了。副词 exhaustively。
注意别混：exhaustive 是「查得彻底」，exhausting 是「累人」。
① The search was exhaustive and the thing was in the car.
= 那次搜寻查得彻彻底底，那东西就在车里。
exhaustive = 详尽无遗的
an exhaustive list = 详尽的清单
② An exhaustive review takes a year and reads like a phone book.
= 一次彻底的评估要一年，读起来像电话簿。
an exhaustive study = 彻底的研究
副词 exhaustively：详尽地、彻底地：修饰研究、检索、讨论的做法。
③ The topic has been exhaustively studied and nobody agrees yet.
= 那个题目已被彻底研究过，可至今没有共识。
be exhaustively studied = 被彻底研究
④ They documented the process exhaustively and lost the folder.
= 他们把流程记录得详尽无遗，然后把文件夹弄丢了。
document something exhaustively = 详尽地记录某事
构词：exhaustive 加 -ly

exhibit
核心：名词与动词。动词一是展出、陈列（作品）；二是（正式）显示出（特征、情绪）；
名词一是展品；二是（美式）展览；三是（法律）证物。
习语 be on exhibit 是正在展出。
① She exhibits twice a year and sells almost nothing at the shows.
= 她一年办两次展，展上几乎卖不出东西。
exhibit something = 展出某物
② The patient exhibits two symptoms and mentions a third.
= 那位病人显出两种症状，自己又提到第三种。
exhibit signs of something = 显出某种迹象
③ The exhibit is behind glass and the label is wrong.
= 那件展品在玻璃后面，说明牌写错了。
an exhibit = 展品
be on exhibit = 正在展出

exhibition
核心：三支。一是展览、展览会：可数；
二是展示、表现（能力或情绪）：an exhibition of temper；
三是（英式）（学院的）奖学金。
习语 make an exhibition of yourself 是当众出丑。
① The exhibition runs for six weeks and the catalogue sells out in two.
= 那个展览办六周，图录两周就卖光了。
an exhibition = 展览
put on an exhibition = 举办展览
② That was an exhibition of temper and everybody pretended to read.
= 那是一场脾气的表演，人人都装作在看书。
an exhibition of something = 某种情绪或本领的表现
③ He made an exhibition of himself at the reception and apologised by letter.
= 他在招待会上出了大丑，事后写信道歉。
make an exhibition of yourself = 当众出丑

exhibitionism
核心：两支。一是爱出风头、卖弄：故意引人注目的做派；
二是（心理学）露阴癖。不可数。
① The speech was exhibitionism with slides and one useful number.
= 那场演讲是配了幻灯的自我卖弄，外加一个有用的数字。
exhibitionism = 爱出风头
② Exhibitionism as a clinical term means something narrower than the insult.
= 作为临床术语，露阴癖的所指比骂人时窄得多。
构词：exhibitionist 加 -ism

exhibitionist
核心：两支。一是爱出风头的人：处处想被看见的那种；
二是（心理学）露阴者。可数，也作形容词。
① The exhibitionist in that group organises everything and thanks nobody.
= 那个圈子里爱出风头的人什么都张罗，谁也不谢。
an exhibitionist = 爱出风头的人
② An exhibitionist streak helps on stage and nowhere else.
= 爱表现的那点性子在台上有用，别处没用。
an exhibitionist streak = 爱表现的性子

exhibitor
核心：参展者、参展商：在展览或展销会上展出作品或产品的人与公司。可数。
① Exhibitors arrive the day before and complain about the loading bay.
= 参展商提前一天到，抱怨的都是卸货区。
an exhibitor = 参展者
② The exhibitor next to us sold out and lent us a table.
= 我们旁边那家参展商卖光了货，还借了张桌子给我们。
exhibitors = 参展商（复数）
构词：exhibit 加 -or

exhilarate
核心：使兴奋、使振奋：说经历让人精神一振，多用被动 be exhilarated。
名词 exhilaration。
① Cold water exhilarates for about a minute and then it is just cold.
= 冷水让人振奋大约一分钟，之后就只剩冷。
exhilarate somebody = 使某人振奋
② The climb exhilarated everybody except the person carrying the ropes.
= 那段攀登让所有人振奋，除了背绳子那位。
be exhilarated = 感到振奋

exhilarated
核心：兴奋振奋的：说人经历之后精神大振、心情高涨。
① They came off the water exhilarated and could not feel their hands.
= 他们从水里上来，兴奋极了，手已经没知觉。
be exhilarated = 兴奋振奋
② An exhilarated crowd is easier to move than a bored one.
= 一群兴奋起来的人，比一群无聊的人好带动。
an exhilarated crowd = 兴奋起来的人群

exhilarating
核心：令人振奋的、痛快的：说经历或场面让人精神一振。
① The first ride is exhilarating and the queue for it is ninety minutes.
= 头一趟坐着痛快极了，排队要九十分钟。
exhilarating = 令人振奋的
an exhilarating ride = 痛快的一程
② Teaching that class is exhilarating and leaves nothing for the evening.
= 教那个班让人振奋，也把晚上的力气全掏空了。
be exhilarating = 令人精神一振

exhilaration
核心：振奋、兴奋：经历刺激或成功之后那股精神一振的感觉。不可数。
① The exhilaration lasted until they had to carry the boat back.
= 那股振奋一直持续到他们得把船抬回去。
exhilaration = 振奋、兴奋
a sense of exhilaration = 一阵振奋
② Exhilaration and relief look the same from a distance.
= 振奋跟松了一口气，远看是一个样。
构词：exhilarate 加 -ion

exhort
核心：（正式）力劝、劝诫：郑重地劝人去做某事 ——
exhort somebody to do something。名词 exhortation。
① The captain exhorted them to run and three of them walked.
= 队长力劝他们跑起来，有三个人在走。
exhort somebody to do something = 力劝某人做某事
② The circular exhorts staff to save paper and is printed in colour.
= 那份通知劝员工省纸，自己是彩色印的。
exhort somebody = 劝诫某人

exhortation
核心：（正式）劝诫、告诫（的话）：郑重劝人做某事的言辞。可数不可数都行。
① The exhortation on the wall has faded and the rule underneath it has not.
= 墙上那句劝诫褪了色，底下那条规矩没有。
an exhortation = 一句劝诫
② Exhortation works for a fortnight and a system works for a year.
= 劝诫管两个星期，一套制度管一年。
exhortation = 劝诫
构词：exhort 加 -ation

exhumation
核心：（正式、法律）掘尸、开棺：把埋葬的遗体挖出来，多为查案或改葬。可数不可数都行。
① The exhumation needed a licence, a tent and two very early mornings.
= 那次开棺要有许可证、一顶帐篷，外加两个大清早。
an exhumation = 一次掘尸
② Exhumation settled a question that had been argued for a century.
= 开棺查验了结了一个争论了一个世纪的问题。
the exhumation of somebody = 对某人遗体的起出

exhume
核心：（正式）掘出（遗体）：把埋着的尸体挖出来；
也比喻把旧事翻出来。多用被动。名词 exhumation。
① The body was exhumed in October and reburied in the same week.
= 那具遗体十月被起出，同一周重新安葬。
be exhumed = 被掘出
exhume a body = 掘出遗体
② He exhumes an argument that everyone had agreed to bury.
= 他把一桩大家说好了埋掉的争论又翻了出来。
exhume something = 把某事重新翻出来

exigency
核心：（正式）紧急情况、迫切需要：非应付不可的形势。
可数为主，复数 exigencies。
① The exigencies of that winter changed what the word rationing meant.
= 那个冬天的紧迫形势，改变了「配给」这个词的含义。
the exigencies of something = 某种形势的迫切要求
② An exigency plan sits in a drawer and gets read once.
= 应急预案躺在抽屉里，被读过一次。
an exigency = 紧急情况

exiguous
核心：（正式，用得少）微薄的、少得可怜的：说数量、收入或供应不足。
① The evidence is exiguous and the conclusion is confident.
= 证据少得可怜，结论倒说得笃定。
exiguous = 微薄的
② They lived on an exiguous income and grew most of what they ate.
= 他们靠一份微薄的收入过日子，吃的多半自己种。
an exiguous income = 微薄的收入

exile
核心：名词两支、动词一支。名词一是流亡、放逐（这件事）：live in exile；
二是流亡者、被放逐的人。动词是把…放逐、流放，多用被动。
① He spent nine years in exile and wrote three of his books there.
= 他流亡了九年，三本书是在那儿写的。
in exile = 在流亡中
exile = 流亡、放逐
② The exiles met in one café and disagreed in four languages.
= 那些流亡者聚在一家咖啡馆，用四种语言争论。
an exile = 流亡者
③ She was exiled to an island with a post office and no bookshop.
= 她被流放到一座有邮局、没书店的岛上。
be exiled = 被流放

exist
核心：三支。一是存在：说事物真有；二是（勉强）生存：exist on very little；
三是（正式）存在于：exist in something。名词 existence，形容词 existing。
习语 cease to exist 是不复存在。
① The rule exists and nobody in the office has read it.
= 那条规矩是存在的，办公室里没人读过。
exist = 存在
② They existed on bread and tea for the last week of every month.
= 每个月最后一周，他们靠面包和茶过活。
exist on something = 靠某物勉强度日
③ The company ceased to exist in March and answers email still.
= 那家公司三月就不复存在了，邮件照样有人回。
cease to exist = 不复存在

existence
核心：三支。一是存在（这件事）：the existence of something；
二是生活、生存方式：a hand-to-mouth existence；
三是 come into existence 是产生、开始存在。不可数为主。
① The existence of the file was denied twice and confirmed by accident.
= 那份档案的存在被否认过两回，后来偶然被证实。
the existence of something = 某物的存在
② They led a hand-to-mouth existence and called it freelancing.
= 他们过着朝不保夕的日子，管这叫自由职业。
a hand-to-mouth existence = 朝不保夕的生活
③ The department came into existence in a corridor conversation.
= 那个部门是在走廊里的一次谈话中诞生的。
come into existence = 产生、开始存在

existent
核心：（正式）存在的、现有的：多用在否定或对比里 ——
barely existent 是几乎不存在的。
① The service is barely existent after seven in the evening.
= 晚上七点以后，那项服务几乎等于没有。
barely existent = 几乎不存在的
② The existent rules cover none of this and the new ones are late.
= 现有的规则一条也管不着这些，新的又迟迟不来。
existent = 现有的、存在的

existential
核心：两支。一是关乎生死存亡的：an existential threat 是生存威胁；
二是（哲学）存在主义的：跟存在与选择的哲学有关。
① The drought is an existential threat to a village of forty people.
= 那场旱灾对一个四十口人的村子，是生死存亡的威胁。
an existential threat = 生存威胁
an existential crisis = 存在危机
② The novel is existential in outlook and comic in tone.
= 那部小说观念上是存在主义的，语调是喜剧的。
existential = 存在主义的

existentialism
核心：（哲学）存在主义：主张人先存在、再由选择造就自身的一路思想，
强调自由与随之而来的责任。不可数。
① Existentialism came out of a war and reads like it.
= 存在主义是从一场战争里出来的，读着也像。
existentialism = 存在主义
② Existentialism gives nobody an excuse and that is the difficult part.
= 存在主义不给任何人借口，难就难在这儿。
French existentialism = 法国存在主义

existentialist
核心：存在主义者：主张存在主义的人；也作形容词。可数。
① The existentialists met in cafés and paid for the coffee separately.
= 那些存在主义者在咖啡馆碰头，咖啡各付各的。
an existentialist = 存在主义者
② An existentialist reading of that play makes the ending harder.
= 用存在主义读法看那出戏，结局更难受。
an existentialist reading = 存在主义式的解读
构词：existentialism 加 -ist

existing
核心：现有的、现存的：说眼下已经有的，只作定语。
注意别混：existing 是「现有的」，existent 更书面，多用于否定对比。
① The existing arrangement works and nobody can explain how.
= 现行的安排管用，谁也说不清是怎么管用的。
the existing arrangement = 现行的安排
existing customers = 老客户
② Existing stock is sold at the old price until Friday.
= 现有的存货按老价钱卖到周五。
existing stock = 现有存货

exit
核心：名词三支、动词两支。名词一是出口：the fire exit；
二是离开、退场：make an exit；三是（高速公路的）出口匝道。
动词一是离开、退出；二是（戏剧指示）（一人）下场。
exit 还能压在别的名词前面，指「离开时的那一下」：an exit poll、an exit interview、
an exit exam、an exit visa、an exit wound。
① The exit is behind the stage and marked in two languages.
= 出口在舞台后面，用两种语言标着。
an exit = 出口
a fire exit = 消防出口
② She made a quiet exit and three people followed her out.
= 她悄悄退了场，有三个人跟着出去了。
make an exit = 退场、离场
③ Take the third exit and the sign for it is behind a tree.
= 走第三个出口，那块指示牌被一棵树挡着。
an exit = 高速出口匝道
④ Exit polls put the margin at four points and the count made it one.
= 出口民调说差四个百分点，点票结果是一个。
an exit poll = 出口民调
an exit interview = 离职面谈
an exit exam = 毕业会考
⑤ Nobody joined that war with an exit strategy and the exit visas took four months.
= 谁卷进那场战争时都没想好退出之计，出境签证又办了四个月。
an exit strategy = 退出之计
an exit visa = 出境签证
⑥ The exit wound told the pathologist rather more than the entry wound did.
= 那处射出口告诉病理学家的，比射入口还多。
an exit wound = 子弹的射出口

exocrine
核心：（生理）外分泌的：说腺体把分泌物经导管送到体表或体腔，
跟内分泌 endocrine 直接进血液相对。只作定语。
① Exocrine glands send their product down a duct and not into the blood.
= 外分泌腺把分泌物经导管送出去，不送进血液。
exocrine glands = 外分泌腺
② Exocrine function is tested separately from the hormone levels.
= 外分泌功能是跟激素水平分开测的。
exocrine function = 外分泌功能

exodus
核心：两支。一是（大批人的）离去、外流：a mass exodus；
二是（大写）《出埃及记》：旧约第二卷。可数为主。
① The exodus started when the mill closed and finished in two years.
= 工厂一关就开始有人走，两年之内走光。
an exodus = 大批离去
a mass exodus = 大规模外流
② Exodus is read at that festival every year and never gets shorter.
= 每年那个节日都要诵读《出埃及记》，也从没变短过。
Exodus = 出埃及记

exogamous
核心：（人类学）外婚的：只能在本群体之外通婚的，跟 endogamous 内婚相对。
① Exogamous rules push marriage outward and keep the peace between villages.
= 外婚的规矩把婚姻推向外面，也维持了村与村之间的和睦。
exogamous rules = 外婚的规矩
② The clan is strictly exogamous and the records go back four generations.
= 那个宗族严格外婚，族谱能上溯四代。
be exogamous = 实行外婚制

exogamy
核心：（人类学）外婚制：必须在本群体之外择偶的制度，跟 endogamy 相对。不可数。
① Exogamy spreads a name across a valley in three generations.
= 外婚制三代人之间，就把一个姓氏散遍整条山谷。
exogamy = 外婚制
② Exogamy and trade tend to travel together.
= 外婚制与贸易，往往结伴而行。
practise exogamy = 实行外婚制

exogenous
核心：（正式、科技）外源的、外生的：由外部来的，跟内源 endogenous 相对 ——
an exogenous shock 是外部冲击。
① An exogenous shock explains the first year and not the next four.
= 外部冲击能解释头一年，解释不了随后四年。
an exogenous shock = 外部冲击
② Exogenous hormones are dosed and the body's own are not.
= 外源激素是按剂量给的，身体自己产的可不是。
exogenous hormones = 外源性激素

exonerate
核心：（正式、法律）免除责任、证明清白：多用被动 ——
be exonerated of something。名词 exoneration。
① The inquiry exonerated the crew and criticised the schedule.
= 调查免除了机组的责任，批评的是排班。
exonerate somebody = 证明某人无责
be exonerated of something = 在某事上被证明清白
② Exonerating one person does not explain what happened.
= 洗清一个人的责任，并没有解释事情是怎么发生的。
exonerate somebody from blame = 免除某人的过错

exoneration
核心：（正式）免责、洗清罪责：证明某人无过失这件事。不可数。
① Exoneration came in a paragraph and the accusation had run for a year.
= 免责说明只有一段，指控闹了一年。
exoneration = 免责、洗清罪责
② Full exoneration is rarer than a quiet withdrawal of the charge.
= 彻底洗清，比悄悄撤销指控还少见。
full exoneration = 彻底洗清

exorbitant
核心：（价格、要求）高得离谱的：说贵得不合理。副词 exorbitantly。
① The rent is exorbitant and the flat has one window.
= 那房租贵得离谱，房子只有一扇窗。
exorbitant = 高得离谱的
an exorbitant price = 离谱的价格
② Exorbitant fees are charged for a form that takes four minutes.
= 一张四分钟就能填完的表，收费高得离谱。
exorbitant fees = 高得离谱的收费
副词 exorbitantly：高得离谱地：多修饰 expensive、priced 一类的词。
③ The tickets are exorbitantly priced and sell out in nine minutes.
= 那些票价高得离谱，九分钟就卖光。
be exorbitantly priced = 定价高得离谱
④ Everything in that shop is exorbitantly expensive and beautifully wrapped.
= 那家店里什么都贵得离谱，包装倒是漂亮。
exorbitantly expensive = 贵得离谱
构词：exorbitant 加 -ly

exorcise
核心：（正式）两支。一是驱邪、驱鬼：用宗教仪式把鬼魂从人或地方赶出去；
二是引申开来，驱散心里挥之不去的痛苦记忆。英式也拼 exorcize。
① The family called in a priest to exorcise the farmhouse after the fire.
= 火灾之后，那家人请了神父来给农舍驱邪。
exorcise a house = 给房子驱邪
exorcise a spirit = 驱走鬼魂
② Writing the memoir was her way of exorcising a childhood she never discussed.
= 写那本回忆录，是她驱散自己从不提起的童年的办法。
exorcise the ghosts of the past = 驱散往事的阴影

exorcism
核心：（宗教）驱魔、驱邪：把鬼魂赶出去的做法，或那一场仪式。
具体一场可数，做法本身不可数。
① The bishop authorised an exorcism only after two doctors had seen the boy.
= 主教是在两位医生看过那男孩之后，才批准举行驱魔礼的。
perform an exorcism = 举行驱魔礼
a rite of exorcism = 驱魔仪式
② Exorcism belongs to the parish's history and not to its weekly routine.
= 驱魔属于这个教区的历史，不属于它每周的日常。
exorcism = 驱邪、驱魔
构词：exorcise 加 -ism

exorcist
核心：驱魔人：受教会指派施行驱魔礼的神职人员。可数。
① The diocese keeps one exorcist and will not give out his name.
= 教区留着一位驱魔人，却不肯透露他的姓名。
call in an exorcist = 请驱魔人来
② Every exorcist in the film is calmer than the family who sent for him.
= 片中每一个驱魔人，都比请他来的那家人镇定。
an exorcist = 一位驱魔人
构词：exorcise 加 -ist

exoskeleton
核心：（生物）外骨骼：昆虫、虾蟹长在体表的硬壳，既支撑身体也当铠甲；
工程上也指套在人身上帮着使劲的机械外骨骼。可数。
① A crab grows by shedding its exoskeleton and hiding until the new shell hardens.
= 螃蟹靠蜕掉外骨骼来长个儿，新壳变硬之前一直躲着。
shed an exoskeleton = 蜕去外骨骼
an exoskeleton = 外骨骼
② Warehouse staff wear a powered exoskeleton that takes the weight off the spine.
= 仓库工人穿着动力外骨骼，替脊椎卸掉重量。
a powered exoskeleton = 动力外骨骼

exothermic
核心：（化学）放热的：反应过程中往外放出热量，跟吸热的那一类相对。
几乎只修饰 reaction、process 这类词。
① The mixture is exothermic, so the flask gets too hot to hold within seconds.
= 这个混合过程会放热，烧瓶几秒钟就烫得握不住。
an exothermic reaction = 放热反应
② Setting concrete is exothermic and a thick pour can crack from the inside.
= 混凝土凝固会放热，浇得太厚会从里面裂开。
exothermic = 放热的

exotic
核心：三支。一是异国情调的、外来的：来自远方，因而显得新奇好看；
二是口语里的「不同寻常、惹眼的」；三是生物学上说物种外来，不带褒贬。
注意语域：如今说人 exotic 常被当成把人看成风景，有冒犯之嫌。
① The market sells exotic fruit that arrives by air twice a week.
= 那个市场卖的异国水果，每周空运来两趟。
exotic fruit = 异国水果
an exotic location = 异域风光的外景地
② His holidays get more exotic every year and his stories get shorter.
= 他的假期一年比一年远走高飞，故事却越讲越短。
exotic = 新奇罕见的
③ Exotic species escape from gardens and settle in the hedgerows.
= 外来物种从花园里跑出来，在树篱里安了家。
an exotic species = 外来物种
④ The exotic dancer on the poster has been there since the club shut in 2011.
= 海报上那位脱衣舞娘，从 2011 年俱乐部关张起就贴在那儿。
an exotic dancer = 脱衣舞娘（委婉说法）
副词 exotically：异国情调地、奇特地：修饰 dressed、decorated、named 这一类词， 说的是打扮或布置看着来自远方。
⑤ The bar is exotically decorated and serves exactly three kinds of beer.
= 那家酒吧装潢得满是异国情调，卖的啤酒总共三种。
exotically decorated = 装潢得富于异国情调
⑥ She arrived exotically dressed and left in a borrowed raincoat.
= 她来时一身异域打扮，走时披着借来的雨衣。
exotically dressed = 穿得富于异国情调
构词：exotic 加 -ly

exotica
核心：（正式）异国珍玩、奇趣之物：成堆来自远方的稀奇东西，作复数看待。
也指五十年代那种带南洋风味的轻音乐。
① The back room is full of exotica bought at auction and never once priced.
= 里屋堆满了拍卖会上买来、从没标过价的异国玩意儿。
exotica = 异国珍玩
a collection of exotica = 一批异域藏品
② Musical exotica from the 1950s has been reissued on vinyl and sells steadily.
= 五十年代那批异国情调音乐出了黑胶再版，卖得一直不错。
musical exotica = 异国情调音乐

exoticism
核心：（正式）异国情调（这种趣味）：把远方文化当新奇景观来欣赏的风格，
多见于评论艺术、时尚与文学。不可数。
① The novel trades on exoticism and gets half the street names wrong.
= 那部小说靠异国情调招徕读者，街名倒错了一半。
exoticism = 异国情调
② Critics praised the exoticism of the costumes and said nothing about the plot.
= 评论家称赞戏服的异域风情，对情节只字不提。
the exoticism of something = 某物的异域风情
构词：exotic 加 -ism

expand
核心：三支。一是膨胀、扩大：说体积、数量、范围变大，及物不及物都行；
二是扩张业务、扩充规模；三是 expand on something，把话展开细说。
反义是 contract；名词 expansion。
① Warm air expands and the roof timbers creak all afternoon.
= 空气受热膨胀，屋顶的木梁整个下午吱吱作响。
expand = 膨胀、扩大
② The firm expanded from one van to a fleet of forty in six years.
= 那家公司六年间从一辆货车扩张成四十辆的车队。
expand into new markets = 打入新市场
③ He was asked to expand on the second paragraph and talked for an hour.
= 有人请他把第二段展开讲讲，他讲了一个钟头。
expand on something = 就某事详加说明
expand upon something = 就某事详加说明（更正式的写法）

expandable
核心：可扩充的、可加大的：多说存储、家具、编制 —— 留了余地，日后能加。
少数场合拼作 expandible。
① The dining table is expandable and seats six once the leaf is pulled out.
= 那张餐桌能加长，把活动板拉出来可以坐六个人。
expandable = 可扩充的
② The camera has expandable storage and a battery that barely lasts a morning.
= 那台相机存储可以扩充，电池却勉强撑一个上午。
expandable memory = 可扩充的内存

expanse
核心：（书面）一大片：说水面、天空、土地一眼望不到边，几乎总跟 of 连用。
可数，前面常配 vast、wide、open。
① A vast expanse of wheat runs from the road to a line of poplars.
= 从公路一直到那排白杨，是一大片望不到头的麦田。
a vast expanse of something = 一大片……
② The window gives onto an expanse of grey water with one boat moored in it.
= 窗外是一片灰色的水面，中间泊着一条船。
an expanse of water = 一片水面

expansion
核心：两支。一是扩张、扩大：规模、数量、领土的增长，常配 rapid、economic；
二是物理上的膨胀，如 thermal expansion。不可数为主，指某一次扩张时可数。
① Rapid expansion left the company with three offices and no filing system.
= 迅猛扩张之后，公司有了三个办事处，却没有一套档案制度。
rapid expansion = 迅猛扩张
economic expansion = 经济扩张
② Thermal expansion opens the joints on the bridge by a few millimetres.
= 热膨胀让桥上的伸缩缝张开了几毫米。
thermal expansion = 热膨胀
构词：expand 加 -sion

expansionary
核心：（经济）扩张性的：指有意刺激增长的财政与货币政策，跟紧缩的相对。
几乎只修饰 policy、budget、phase 这类词。
① The budget is expansionary and the central bank has said so twice this month.
= 这份预算是扩张性的，央行这个月已经说过两回。
expansionary fiscal policy = 扩张性财政政策
② Expansionary policy pulls unemployment down and pushes the rents up.
= 扩张性政策把失业率压下去，把房租推上来。
expansionary policy = 扩张性政策

expansionism
核心：（贬）扩张主义：以扩大领土或势力范围为目标的那套主张。不可数，
多见于政治评论；说人则用 expansionist。
① Nineteenth-century expansionism is taught here as settlement and elsewhere as conquest.
= 十九世纪的扩张主义，这里当拓殖来教，别处当征服来教。
expansionism = 扩张主义
② Territorial expansionism turns expensive long before it turns popular.
= 领土扩张主义早在得人心之前，就已经很烧钱了。
territorial expansionism = 领土扩张主义
构词：expansion 加 -ism

expansionist
核心：扩张主义的、扩张主义者：形容词与名词同形，多含贬义。
形容词修饰 policy、wing、ambition；名词说人。
① The party's expansionist wing lost the vote and kept the newspaper.
= 该党主张扩张的那一翼输了表决，倒是保住了报纸。
expansionist policies = 扩张主义政策
② Expansionists inside the ministry argued for a second port on the north coast.
= 部里的扩张派主张在北岸再修一个港口。
an expansionist = 扩张主义者

expansive
核心：两支。一是开阔的、大范围的：说视野、手势、理解的宽窄；
二是说人谈兴大发，吃饱喝足之后话多起来，in expansive mood。
副词 expansively，名词 expansiveness。
① The terrace has an expansive view of the estuary and no shade at all.
= 那片露台望得见开阔的河口，却一点遮阴也没有。
an expansive view = 开阔的视野
② After two glasses he grew expansive about his years at sea.
= 两杯下肚，他谈兴大发，讲起自己出海的那些年。
in expansive mood = 谈兴正浓
③ The report takes an expansive view of what counts as a household.
= 那份报告对什么算一户人家，取的是宽泛的理解。
an expansive definition = 宽泛的定义
副词 expansively：两支。一是大幅度地：多跟 gesture、wave 连用，形容手一挥的架势； 二是兴致高地、滔滔不绝地，说的是讲话时的那副样子。
④ He waved expansively at the fields and mentioned none of the debts.
= 他朝田野大手一挥，一句债务也没提。
gesture expansively = 大幅度地做手势
⑤ She talked expansively about next season and briefly about this one.
= 她大谈下个赛季，对本赛季只说了几句。
talk expansively = 滔滔不绝地讲
构词：expansive 加 -ly

expansiveness
核心：两支。一是辽阔、开阔：地方或视野的宽广；
二是热情健谈的劲头，说人放开了谈的那种状态。不可数。
① The sheer expansiveness of the plain is hard to photograph from a car window.
= 那片平原到底有多辽阔，从车窗里很难拍出来。
the sheer expansiveness of something = 某物的辽阔
② His expansiveness at dinner surprised colleagues who had only ever emailed him.
= 他饭桌上那股健谈劲头，让只跟他通过邮件的同事很意外。
expansiveness = 热情健谈

expansivity
核心：（物理）膨胀率：材料受热之后胀多少，专业行文里写作 thermal expansivity。
不可数。日常说法是 expansion。
① Steel and glass differ in expansivity, so the frames crack in a hard frost.
= 钢和玻璃的膨胀率不一样，重霜天里窗框就会开裂。
expansivity = 膨胀率
② Low expansivity is why the dish survives both the oven and the cold tap.
= 正因为膨胀率低，那只盘子进烤箱、冲冷水都没事。
thermal expansivity = 热膨胀率

expat
核心：（非正式）侨居国外的人：expatriate 的截短形，现在比全称还常用。
也当形容词，说 expat workers、the expat community。
① The bar fills with expats on Fridays and with students the rest of the week.
= 那家酒吧周五挤满了外派侨民，别的日子都是学生。
the expat community = 侨民圈子
② She has been an expat for eleven years and still votes by post.
= 她在国外住了十一年，投票还是靠邮寄。
an expat = 侨居国外的人

expatiate
核心：（正式，常带调侃）长篇大论、详说：几乎只用 expatiate on 或 upon something，
言下之意是讲得比听众想听的多。
① The chairman expatiated on the history of the site until the caterers arrived.
= 主席就那块地的来历长篇大论，一直讲到送餐的人来了。
expatiate on something = 就某事长篇大论
② Given half a chance he will expatiate upon the merits of the older engine.
= 只要给一点机会，他就会大谈老款发动机的种种好处。
expatiate upon something = 详论某事

expatriate
核心：三支。一是名词，侨居国外的人，尤指外派去工作的，口语截短成 expat；
二是形容词，expatriate staff、expatriate community；
三是（正式）动词，把人逐出国、或自己放弃本国国籍，多用被动。
① The company hires local staff and two expatriates who rotate every three years.
= 公司雇本地员工，另有两名外派人员，每三年轮换一次。
an expatriate = 侨居国外的人
② Expatriate teachers fill the timetable that the island cannot staff itself.
= 外籍教师顶上了本岛自己排不满的课表。
expatriate staff = 外派员工
③ Those who were expatriated in 1946 were later told they could apply to return.
= 1946 年被逐出国的那些人，后来被告知可以申请回来。
be expatriated = 被逐出国境

expect
核心：四支。一是预料、料想会发生：expect that、expect to do something；
二是等着某人某物到来；三是要求、指望某人做某事，带点应尽义务的意思；
四是 be expecting，委婉说怀孕了。反问句 what do you expect 是摊手的口气。
① We expect the results on Friday, so nobody has booked anything for the weekend.
= 我们预计周五出结果，所以周末谁都没安排别的事。
expect something = 预料某事
expect to do something = 预计要做某事
② She is expecting a parcel and has left a note for the courier on the door.
= 她在等一个包裹，门上给快递员留了张条。
be expecting somebody = 在等某人来
③ The school expects parents to read every letter it sends home.
= 学校指望家长把发回家的每一封信都读一遍。
expect somebody to do something = 指望某人做某事
④ They are expecting again, which is why the spare room is being painted.
= 他们又要添孩子了，那间空房才在粉刷。
be expecting = 有喜了
⑤ He turned up late and blamed the traffic — what do you expect from a Friday?
= 他迟到了，怪路上堵 —— 周五还能指望什么呢？
what do you expect = 你还指望什么呢

expectancy
核心：两支。一是期待：等着好事发生的那股气氛，an air of expectancy；
二是统计上的预期值，最常见的说法是 life expectancy 平均寿命。不可数。
① An air of expectancy filled the hall before either candidate had spoken.
= 两位候选人还没开口，大厅里就弥漫着一股期待。
an air of expectancy = 一片期待的气氛
② Life expectancy here rose by nine years and then stopped rising.
= 这里的平均寿命涨了九岁，之后就不再涨了。
life expectancy = 平均预期寿命

expectant
核心：两支。一是满怀期待的：expectant faces，脸上写着等好事；
二是固定说法 an expectant mother，指孕妇。副词 expectantly。
① Forty expectant faces turned towards the door and the wrong man walked in.
= 四十张满怀期待的脸转向门口，进来的却是别人。
expectant faces = 一张张期待的脸
② Expectant mothers are seen in the side clinic and never wait long.
= 孕妇在旁边那间诊室看诊，从来不用等太久。
an expectant mother = 孕妇
副词 expectantly：期待地、眼巴巴地：多修饰 look、wait、glance，说的是等回应的那副神情。
③ The dog sat expectantly by the cupboard where the leads are kept.
= 那条狗满怀期待地坐在放牵引绳的柜子旁边。
wait expectantly = 满怀期待地等着
④ She looked at him expectantly and he went on reading the menu.
= 她眼巴巴地看着他，他继续读他的菜单。
look expectantly at somebody = 期待地看着某人
构词：expectant 加 -ly

expectation
核心：期望、预期：既指「以为会发生」，也指「要求别人达到的标准」，
后一支多用复数 expectations。常见搭配 live up to expectations、
exceed expectations，以及 contrary to expectation。
① Contrary to expectation, the older bridge took the flood and the new one did not.
= 出乎意料的是，扛住洪水的是那座老桥，不是新桥。
contrary to expectation = 出乎意料
② The film exceeded expectations at home and disappointed everyone abroad.
= 那部电影在国内超出预期，在国外让所有人失望。
exceed expectations = 超出预期
③ Nobody can live up to expectations that were never said out loud.
= 从没说出口的期望，谁也达不到。
live up to expectations = 不负期望
④ Expectation of life in those tables is worked out for a whole cohort, not for you.
= 那些表上的预期寿命，是按整代人算的，不是按你算的。
expectation of life = 预期寿命

expected
核心：预期的、意料之中的：多作定语，如 the expected outcome；
副词化的 as expected 是「果然」。跟 unexpected 相对。
① The expected crowd never came and the stewards folded the barriers away.
= 预计的人潮一直没来，工作人员把围栏收了起来。
the expected outcome = 预期的结果
② As expected, the cheapest quote came with the longest delivery time.
= 果然，报价最低的那家交货期最长。
as expected = 果然、不出所料

expectorant
核心：（医学）祛痰药：帮人把痰咳出来的药，也作形容词修饰 syrup、mixture。可数。
① The pharmacist recommended an expectorant and told him to drink more water.
= 药师给他推荐了一种祛痰药，还叫他多喝水。
an expectorant = 一种祛痰药
② Expectorant syrups taste of aniseed and are cheaper than the branded pills.
= 祛痰糖浆一股茴香味，比那些名牌药片便宜。
an expectorant syrup = 祛痰糖浆

expectorate
核心：（正式、医学）咳痰、吐痰：日常说 spit，只有告示牌与病历上写这个词。
名词 expectoration。
① A sign on the platform still asks passengers not to expectorate.
= 站台上还挂着一块牌子，请乘客不要随地吐痰。
expectorate = 吐痰、咳出痰
② Patients are asked to expectorate into a pot before the sample is sealed.
= 采样封存之前，医生请病人把痰咳进小盒里。
expectorate into something = 把痰咳进某容器

expectoration
核心：（医学）咳痰：既指咳的这个动作，也指咳出来的那口痰。不可数。
① Expectoration at night is the symptom the doctor asked about twice.
= 夜里咳痰，是医生问了两遍的那个症状。
expectoration = 咳痰
② Blood in the expectoration sent him back to the clinic that same afternoon.
= 痰里带血，让他当天下午又回了诊所。
blood in the expectoration = 痰中带血
构词：expectorate 加 -ion

expediency
核心：（多含贬义）权宜、只图管用：眼下方便就好，不问是否合乎原则。
不可数，最常见的搭配是 political expediency。异体 expedience 少见。
① The rule was dropped for political expediency and quietly restored in June.
= 那条规矩出于政治权宜被取消，六月又悄悄恢复。
political expediency = 政治上的权宜
② Expediency explains the timetable and does not excuse it.
= 权宜之计解释得了这份时间表，却开脱不了它。
expediency = 权宜行事

expedient
核心：两支。一是形容词，权宜的、图省事的：多用 it is expedient to do something，
言下之意是管用但未必正当；二是名词，应急的权宜之计。副词 expediently。
① The committee found it expedient to postpone the vote until after the holiday.
= 委员会觉得把表决推到假期之后比较省事。
it is expedient to do something = 做某事比较权宜
② Borrowing from next year's budget is an expedient, not a plan.
= 挪用明年的预算是权宜之计，不是计划。
an expedient = 权宜之计
副词 expediently：权宜地、图方便地：修饰处理方式，暗示图的是省事，不是公正。
③ The complaint was handled expediently and nobody looked at the paperwork.
= 那桩投诉图省事就打发了，没人翻过那些材料。
handle something expediently = 图省事地处理某事
④ Acting expediently in March cost the department the whole of April.
= 三月里图省事，让那个部门赔上了整个四月。
act expediently = 权宜行事
构词：expedient 加 -ly

expedite
核心：（正式）加快、加急办理：让手续或流程走得快些。及物，
宾语多是 process、delivery、application 这类词，公文与商务信里常见。
① A second signature would expedite the transfer and nobody can find the registrar.
= 再补一个签名就能加快转账，可谁也找不到那位登记员。
expedite the process = 加快流程
② Paying the extra fee expedites delivery by exactly one working day.
= 多付那笔加急费，交货正好提前一个工作日。
expedite delivery = 加急发货

expedition
核心：两支。一是远征、探险、科考（也指那支队伍）：go on an expedition；
二是（正式）迅速、麻利，只见于 with all expedition 这类说法。
① The expedition set out with four sledges and came back with only two.
= 那支考察队带着四架雪橇出发，回来时只剩两架。
go on an expedition = 参加一次远征
an expedition to the interior = 深入内陆的考察
② The court asked that the papers be filed with all expedition.
= 法庭要求尽速把文件递交上去。
with all expedition = 尽速、火速

expeditious
核心：（正式）迅速而有效率的：多修饰 manner、resolution、response，
一股公文腔，日常场合说 quick。副词 expeditiously。
① The tenant asked for an expeditious repair and got a letter about the rules.
= 租客要求尽快修好，收到的却是一封讲规矩的信。
in an expeditious manner = 以迅速的方式
② An expeditious hearing suits everyone except the side that is stalling.
= 迅速开庭对谁都好，只除了那个在拖延的一方。
expeditious = 迅速高效的
副词 expeditiously：迅速地、麻利地：法律文书与公文里的常用词，多跟 deal with、handle、act 连用。
③ Claims under a thousand pounds are dealt with expeditiously and without a hearing.
= 一千镑以下的索赔从速处理，也不开庭。
deal with something expeditiously = 从速处理某事
④ The bank promised to act expeditiously and then took eleven weeks.
= 银行答应尽快办理，结果办了十一个星期。
act expeditiously = 迅速行事
构词：expeditious 加 -ly

expel
核心：两支。一是开除、驱逐：把学生赶出学校、把人逐出国境，
多用被动 be expelled from something；二是排出气体或液体。
变形双写 l：expelled、expelling。名词 expulsion。
① Two players were expelled from the tournament and the third was only fined.
= 有两名选手被逐出赛事，第三个只罚了款。
expel somebody from something = 把某人逐出某处
be expelled from school = 被学校开除
② The whale expels a spout of air that can be heard from the cliff top.
= 鲸鱼喷出一股气，站在崖顶都听得见。
expel air = 排出空气

expend
核心：（正式）花费、耗用：说时间、精力、金钱用在某事上，
搭配 expend something on something。比 spend 正式，多见于书面。名词 expenditure。
① They expend more energy on the rota than on the work it covers.
= 他们花在排班表上的精力，比花在班上那些活儿还多。
expend something on something = 把某物耗在某事上
② Half the grant was expended before a single wall came down.
= 一堵墙还没拆，拨款就已经用掉一半。
be expended = 被耗用掉

expendable
核心：可牺牲的、用完即弃的：说人时相当冷酷，意思是没了也不要紧；
说物则指一次性的耗材。
① The plan treats the night shift as expendable and says so in a footnote.
= 那份方案把夜班工人当成可以牺牲的，还写在脚注里。
be treated as expendable = 被当成可牺牲的
② Expendable parts are ordered by the box and never repaired.
= 易耗件是整箱订的，坏了从不修。
expendable parts = 易耗零件

expenditure
核心：（正式）支出、开销：花出去的钱，也说精力的耗费。
不可数为主，分项列举时可数；跟 income 相对。
① Public expenditure rose in every department except the one under review.
= 除了正在被审查的那个部门，各部门的公共开支都涨了。
public expenditure = 公共开支
capital expenditure = 资本支出
② The expenditure of effort was enormous and the result fitted on one page.
= 耗掉的精力极大，结果只装了一页纸。
the expenditure of effort = 精力的耗费

expense
核心：两支。一是花费、开销，不可数；二是复数 expenses，办事产生的、
可以报销的费用。要紧的是两个固定搭配：at the expense of something 是
以牺牲某事为代价，at somebody's expense 既指某人掏钱，也指拿某人取笑。
① The hall was restored at great expense and is booked eleven days a year.
= 那座礼堂花大钱修复，一年只租出去十一天。
go to the expense of doing something = 不惜花钱去做某事
spare no expense = 不惜工本
② He got the deal through at the expense of everyone who worked weekends.
= 他把那笔交易促成了，代价是所有周末加班的人。
at the expense of something = 以牺牲某事为代价
③ The joke was at his expense and he laughed longest of all.
= 那个玩笑是拿他打趣的，他自己笑得最久。
at somebody's expense = 拿某人取笑
④ Travel expenses are paid monthly and the receipts have to be stapled together.
= 差旅费按月报销，收据必须钉在一起。
travel expenses = 差旅费
⑤ Every lunch went on the expense account and the receipts stopped in March.
= 每一顿午饭都记在公费账上，收据到三月就断了。
an expense account = 公费报销账户

expensive
核心：贵的、花钱多的：既说价格高，也说代价大，如 an expensive mistake。
反义 cheap 与 inexpensive；副词 expensively。
① The camera is expensive to buy and surprisingly cheap to run.
= 那台相机买着贵，用起来倒便宜得出奇。
be expensive to do something = 做某事花费高
② Ignoring the survey turned out to be an expensive mistake.
= 不理会那份验房报告，结果成了代价高昂的错误。
an expensive mistake = 代价高昂的错误
③ Expensive tastes are easy to acquire on somebody else's holiday.
= 花着别人的钱度假，很容易养出奢侈的口味。
expensive tastes = 奢侈的品味
副词 expensively：昂贵地、花大钱地：多修饰 dressed、furnished、educated， 常带一点「钱花了未必值」的口气。
④ The lobby is expensively furnished and the lifts break every winter.
= 那个大堂装修得很花钱，电梯每年冬天都坏。
expensively furnished = 装修得很昂贵
⑤ He was expensively educated and still cannot boil an egg.
= 他上学花了大钱，到现在连鸡蛋都不会煮。
expensively educated = 受过昂贵教育的
构词：expensive 加 -ly

experience
核心：三支。一是经验、阅历（不可数）：work experience、from experience；
二是一次经历（可数）：a memorable experience；三是动词，亲身经历、感受到，
多见于 experience pain、experience difficulty 这类正式说法。形容词 experienced。
① She has ten years in the trade and knows from experience which suppliers ring back.
= 她入行十年，凭经验就知道哪些供应商会回电话。
work experience = 工作经验
from experience = 凭经验
② In my experience the second week of a strike changes the tone of the talks.
= 依我的经验，罢工到第二周，谈判的口气就变了。
in my experience = 依我的经验
③ Sleeping in the airport was an experience she describes but never recommends.
= 在机场过夜是一次经历，她讲归讲，从来不推荐。
an experience = 一次经历
④ Patients experience the pain differently depending on how the news was broken.
= 病人对疼痛的感受各不相同，要看消息是怎么告诉他们的。
experience pain = 感到疼痛
⑤ The game hands out experience points for walking about and none for thinking.
= 那款游戏走来走去就给经验值，动脑子一分不给。
experience points = 经验值

experienced
核心：有经验的、老练的：多作定语，如 an experienced nurse；
也说 be experienced in something。反义 inexperienced。
① An experienced skipper reads the sky and leaves the forecast on the table.
= 老练的船长看天色，把天气预报撂在桌上。
an experienced teacher = 有经验的老师
② She is experienced in negotiating with landlords and hates every minute of it.
= 她很有跟房东打交道的经验，可每一分钟都讨厌。
be experienced in something = 在某方面有经验

experiential
核心：（正式、教育学）体验式的、从亲身经历得来的：最常见的说法是
experiential learning，指靠动手做、不靠听讲来学。
① The course is experiential and the first lecture happens in week four.
= 这门课是体验式的，第一场讲座排在第四周。
experiential learning = 体验式学习
② Experiential knowledge is hard to write down and easy to demonstrate.
= 亲身经历得来的知识难写下来，演示起来倒容易。
experiential knowledge = 体验得来的知识

experiment
核心：名词与动词同形。名词是实验、试验，搭配 carry out 或 conduct an experiment；
动词是做实验、试新做法，搭配 experiment with something。
名词还有 experimentation，形容词 experimental。
① The class carried out an experiment with two candles and a jam jar.
= 那个班用两支蜡烛和一个果酱瓶做了个实验。
carry out an experiment = 做实验
conduct an experiment = 进行实验
② The bakery is experimenting with rye and has stopped taking phone orders.
= 那家面包房正在试着用黑麦，已经不接电话订单了。
experiment with something = 拿某物做尝试

experimental
核心：两支。一是实验的、试验性的：还在试用阶段，没定型；
二是（艺术上）先锋的、不照常规来的。副词 experimentally。
① The drug is still experimental and is given only under a hospital licence.
= 那种药还在试验阶段，只在医院许可下才用。
an experimental treatment = 试验性疗法
on an experimental basis = 以试验的方式
② Her experimental films have no dialogue and a very loyal audience.
= 她那些先锋电影没有对白，观众倒非常忠实。
experimental music = 先锋音乐
副词 experimentally：两支。一是试验性地：先小规模试试看，成了再推开； 二是试探地：动手时小心翼翼地试一下。
③ The route was run experimentally for a term and then written into the timetable.
= 那条线路试运行了一个学期，之后写进了正式时刻表。
run something experimentally = 试运行某事
④ He pressed the swollen ankle experimentally and decided against the walk.
= 他试探着按了按肿起来的脚踝，决定不去走那趟路了。
press something experimentally = 试探着按一按
构词：experimental 加 -ly

experimentation
核心：（正式）反复试验（这件事）：一遍遍试出办法的过程。不可数，
常配 years of experimentation、animal experimentation。
① Years of experimentation went into a recipe that takes nine minutes to cook.
= 一道九分钟就能做好的菜，背后是多年的反复试验。
years of experimentation = 多年的反复试验
② Experimentation on animals is licensed here and reported every spring.
= 这里做动物实验须经许可，每年春天还要上报。
animal experimentation = 动物实验

experimenter
核心：实验者、爱折腾新做法的人：既说实验室里的研究人员，也说凡事爱试一试的人。可数。
① The experimenter leaves the room and the recording keeps running.
= 实验人员离开房间，录音一直开着。
an experimenter = 实验者
② He is a lifelong experimenter and a very poor keeper of notes.
= 他一辈子爱试验，做笔记却糟糕得很。
a lifelong experimenter = 一辈子爱试验的人
构词：experiment 加 -er

expert
核心：三支。一是名词，专家：an expert on something 说的是研究某题目的专家，
an expert in something 说的是某个行当里的行家，an expert at doing something
则是做某件事的能手；二是形容词，内行的、专家给的；
三是引申用法 an expert eye、an expert hand，说的是行家的眼力与手艺。
名词 expertise，副词 expertly。
① She is an expert on medieval roofs and gets called out to three counties.
= 她是研究中世纪屋顶的专家，三个郡都请她去看。
an expert on something = 某方面的专家
an expert in something = 某个行当里的行家
an expert at doing something = 做某事的行家
② The report was sent for expert advice and came back with two sentences changed.
= 那份报告送去征求专家意见，回来只改了两句话。
expert advice = 专家意见
③ It takes an expert eye to tell the copy from the original at this distance.
= 隔这么远要分出赝品和真迹，得有行家的眼力。
an expert eye = 行家的眼力
④ The expert system asks forty questions and passes the hard ones to a person.
= 那套专家系统问四十个问题，难的仍旧转给人处理。
an expert system = 专家系统
副词 expertly：熟练地、老到地：多修饰 handled、boned、taken、played，说的是手上的功夫。
⑤ The fishmonger expertly boned the trout while answering two phone calls.
= 鱼贩一边接两个电话，一边熟练地给鳟鱼剔骨。
expertly handled = 处理得老到
⑥ The corner was expertly taken and then headed straight over the bar.
= 那个角球开得很老到，头球却顶过了横梁。
expertly taken = 开得漂亮
构词：expert 加 -ly

expertise
核心：专门知识、行家本领：靠长期训练练出来的那一手。不可数 ——
不说 an expertise，要说 a level of expertise 或 technical expertise。
① The firm has the expertise and not the equipment, so the work goes abroad.
= 那家公司有本事没设备，活儿只好送到国外做。
technical expertise = 技术专长
have the expertise to do something = 有做某事的本事
② Her expertise in dating timber settled an argument that had run for years.
= 她在木材断代上的专长，了结了一场吵了好些年的争论。
expertise in something = 某方面的专长

expiate
核心：（正式、书面）赎罪、补过：拿行动去抵消自己犯下的过错。
及物，宾语多是 sin、guilt、crime。名词 expiation。
① He spent thirty years trying to expiate a decision made in one afternoon.
= 一个下午做的决定，他花了三十年去赎。
expiate a sin = 赎罪
② Nothing in the settlement expiates the delay, and the family says so plainly.
= 那笔和解金抵不了拖延的过错，那家人也把话挑明了。
expiate guilt = 补赎罪愆

expiation
核心：（正式、宗教）赎罪、补赎：用苦行或善举去抵过错这件事。不可数。
① The pilgrimage began as expiation and turned into an annual holiday.
= 那趟朝圣起初是为赎罪，后来变成了一年一度的假期。
expiation = 赎罪
② Expiation of the debt took two harvests and most of the herd.
= 抵清那笔债，花了两季收成，还搭上了大半群牲口。
expiation of something = 对某事的补赎
构词：expiate 加 -ion

expiration
核心：两支。一是（多为美式，正式）期满、到期：合同或证件失效；
二是（医学）呼气，跟吸气相对。英式日常说 expiry。
① On the expiration of the lease the shop reverts to the family trust.
= 租约期满，那家店就归还给家族信托。
the expiration of a contract = 合同期满
an expiration date = 有效期限
② Each expiration is measured through a tube and printed onto a paper slip.
= 每一次呼气都通过一根管子测量，打印在小纸条上。
expiration = 呼气

expire
核心：两支。一是（证件、合同、期限）到期失效：the passport has expired；
二是（书面、委婉）咽气、去世。名词是 expiry 与 expiration，形容词 expired。
① Her passport expired in March and the appointment is not until June.
= 她的护照三月就到期了，预约却排到六月。
expire = 到期失效
a licence expires = 执照到期
② The old man expired at home with the curtains open and the radio on.
= 老人在家中咽了气，窗帘开着，收音机响着。
expire = 咽气、去世

expired
核心：过期的、失效的：多作定语，如 an expired ticket；
美式口语里也说 be expired，英式则说 has expired。
① The guard waved through an expired pass and lost his job over it.
= 门卫放行了一张过期的通行证，为这事丢了工作。
an expired passport = 过期的护照
② Expired medicines are collected by the pharmacy twice a year.
= 过期药品由药房每年回收两次。
expired medicines = 过期药品

expiry
核心：（英式）期满、到期：证件或合同失效这件事，不可数。
最常用的说法是 the expiry date；美式多说 expiration。
① The expiry of the contract caught both sides without a plan.
= 合同到期，两边都没备下一步的打算。
the expiry of something = 某物的期满
② The expiry date on the packet has been rubbed off by the freezer.
= 包装上的保质期日期让冰柜蹭掉了。
the expiry date = 保质期、有效期

explain
核心：三支。一是解释、说明：explain something to somebody ——
介词用 to，不能说 explain somebody something；二是说明原因、解释得通；
三是两个习语：explain yourself 是把自己的所作所为交代清楚，
explain away 是把不利的事轻描淡写地圆过去。名词 explanation。
① Could you explain the charge to me before I sign anything at all?
= 我签任何字之前，你能不能把这笔费用解释给我听？
explain something to somebody = 向某人解释某事
